## 每日摘要 - 2026-10-16

### Diffusion / 生成式AI

- 论文数：28

**今日推荐精读 Top 3**

1. [SoftCache: Adaptive Step Size Caching for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369v1)
   - 分类：Diffusion Inference Acceleration / Activation Caching
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：扩散模型推理加速的核心效率工作，可与 DaTMe、Speculative Forcing、Velox 等免训练加速方法并列比较，也是对动态缓存机制的直接升级。
   - 痛点：扩散与流模型依赖多步迭代生成样本，每一步都要对大型网络做一次完整前向，生成成本几乎全部由这一项主导。免训练缓存通过复用近期激活来加速，但现有动态缓存方法做的是硬缓存：它把网络输入的廉价探针累积成陈旧度信号，一旦越过阈值就做一次真正的前向，否则直接复用，本质只是一个二值开关；而且无论刷新步还是缓存步，都严格按照预先设定的离散步长推进。因此探针信号中除开关之外的丰富信息被完全浪费，刷新临近时步长本可收缩的自由度也未被利用，加速比与保真度之间的权衡被人为限制。
   - 方法：SoftCache 的核心洞察是探针信号离阈值的余量（headroom）能够预测复用所引入的误差。它据此把硬缓存改成软缓存：余量不再只触发二值切换，而是直接决定每个缓存步可以前进多远，余量大时采样器跳过若干个预定的采样节点，越接近刷新点步长越收缩回原始步长。这些自适应跳跃允许落在预定节点之外，使采样器脱离固定网格，同时方法完全免训练、不引入任何需要按骨干网络单独设置的超参，可直接插入现有的 DiT 与流匹配采样流程。
   - 结果：在图像模型 FLUX 以及视频模型 HunyuanVideo、Wan2.1 上，SoftCache 在 3.5 倍到 6.9 倍的加速区间内，于全部四项保真度指标上都超过了所有对比基线，包括各类免训练缓存与动态缓存方法。结果说明把缓存决策从二值开关升级为连续步长控制，可以在几乎不牺牲生成质量的前提下换取更高的实际加速比。由于无需训练与逐骨干调参，该策略具备较强的即插即用价值。

2. [Gen4P: Video Generators are 4D Perceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22371v1)
   - 分类：4D Perception / Video Generation Foundation Models
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：代表生成式预训练向几何感知迁移的范式，与 GeoState、Velox、P3-WAM 共同构成视频生成先验复用于世界建模的路线。
   - 痛点：从单目视频中恢复 4D 几何与运动是三维视觉的长期目标，但现有方法要么依赖逐场景优化或迭代式去噪，计算昂贵且难以泛化，要么只能处理单一任务，例如只做深度估计或只做点跟踪，无法形成统一框架。与此同时，视频生成模型的预训练权重中蕴含了丰富的时空先验，却主要被用于生成像素而非提取几何。如何把这种生成式预训练知识转化成一个通用的、前馈式的 4D 感知能力，同时避免逐任务重训练与多阶段迭代，仍缺乏系统性方案。
   - 方法：Gen4P 提出统一框架，把预训练视频生成器改造成前馈 4D 感知器：不做迭代去噪，而是在一次编码器前向中处理干净的视频 latent，再通过轻量 cross-attention 解码器按需查询所需的 4D 属性。按需查询的建模方式支持灵活的空间采样，作者进一步提出连续时间查询以获得时间维度上的灵活性。此外，方法引入一个冻结的视频深度 LoRA 作为几何先验插件，在不增加任何可训练参数的前提下增强模型的几何知识，使生成器与判别式几何任务之间的能力迁移更顺畅。
   - 结果：Gen4P 在 3D 点跟踪、视频深度以及相机位姿估计任务上取得 SOTA 或与之相当的性能，在野外视频中还能在跟踪复杂运动的同时保持精细结构不被破坏。这些结果共同说明，视频生成式预训练可以作为统一 4D 感知的有效基础，为把生成模型复用于几何理解提供了可行且高效的范式。方法不依赖逐任务的迭代优化，因而在推理效率上也具备明显优势。

3. [Routing Policy Optimization for Online Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22282v1)
   - 分类：Diffusion RL / Post-training Alignment
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：面向扩散模型在线 RL 后训练的效率问题，与 SCOPE、AVC-Reward、DetNO 同属用强化学习或采样目标改进生成模型的方向。
   - 痛点：用强化学习把视觉生成器对齐到预训练目标之外的奖励信号时，现有 score-function 方法通常通过对反向去噪轨迹加噪来引入随机性，这导致优化必须依赖昂贵的多步 rollout，训练效率低、方差大。同时探索过程发生在采样器内部又会破坏原有的确定性采样流程，使得训练后模型难以保持原有的生成质量与推理开销。因此，如何在保留生成器原生采样器的前提下引入有效随机探索，是扩散 RL 后训练的关键瓶颈。
   - 方法：RPO 把注意力路由变成随机探索动作：在少数选定的去噪层级上，每个 query block 保留自身 block，并按生成器原生注意力质量采样额外的 key block，或退化为稠密注意力。采样到的路由会扰动该步的速度场，并用稠密速度附近的高斯似然以 score-function 目标为该扰动打分。作者进一步设计受控的双 rollout 估计器，两次 rollout 共享 prompt、初始 latent、路由层级与稠密锚层，只在路由上不同，用奖励差为被路由的转移构造优势信号。
   - 结果：由于似然对稠密速度本身可微，学到的更新在推理时移除路由后依然有效，推理阶段可恢复稠密注意力并保留原始确定性采样器。在文生图与文生视频任务上，RPO 在提升生成质量的同时，训练效率比 Flow-GRPO 类方法提高近 4 倍。这表明把探索搬进注意力路由，是一种既高效又不破坏原采样流程的扩散 RL 训练范式。

**主要痛点**
- 扩散/流模型的多步迭代推理成本高，缓存、token 合并、注意力路由与草稿接受等免训练加速方法仍受保真度与加速比权衡限制。
- 视频与世界模型在长时序 roll-out 中 KV cache 持续增长，且视角依赖的几何特征把场景动力学与相机变化纠缠，预测难度大。
- 生成模型缺少细粒度、可解释且与人类对齐的验证与奖励信号，3D 与音视频场景的二值评测无法定位失败原因。
- 强化学习后训练扩散模型依赖昂贵 rollout 与轨迹加噪，破坏原生确定性采样器且训练效率低。

**方法趋势**
- 软缓存式自适应步长控制、动力学感知 token 合并、投机式 token 提前接受、视锥 slab 注意力路由等免训练加速机制。
- 把预训练视频生成器改造为前馈 4D 感知器或 world action model，并引入视角解耦几何状态与可学习环境 latent。
- 注意力路由作为随机探索动作的扩散 RL、语义片段加权的 GRPO prompt 优化、事件为中心的分级验证器与奖励模型。
- flow matching 生成完整轨迹路径、RKHS 体积最大化提升批次多样性，以及针对连续扩散语言模型的架构重设计。

**结果信号**
- SoftCache 在 FLUX 与 HunyuanVideo、Wan2.1 上实现 3.5 到 6.9 倍加速且四项保真度指标全面领先。
- Gen4P 在 3D 点跟踪、视频深度与相机位姿估计上取得 SOTA 或相当性能；LangDiT 把连续语言生成 GenPPL 降至 21.72 与 11.33。
- RPO 训练效率比 Flow-GRPO 类方法高近 4 倍；Speculative Forcing 在 LongLive 上以 1.7 步/token 达到四步生成器质量并快 1.5 倍以上。
- Velox 在 chunk 48 时注意力加速 4.36 倍、去噪 2.43 倍；T2-z 把物体预测误差从 15.20 降到 7.00、动作成功率从 33.73% 升到 72.96%。

### 高效训练与推理

- 论文数：109

**今日推荐精读 Top 3**

1. [SoftCache: Adaptive Step Size Caching for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369v1)
   - 分类：Diffusion Acceleration / Training-free Caching
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：与高效推理方向高度相关，是免训练扩散缓存加速的最新SOTA，可直接用于图像与视频生成部署。
   - 痛点：扩散与流模型生成需经多次迭代，每次都是对大型网络的完整前向，主导了生成成本；现有免训练动态缓存方法采用“硬缓存”，即陈旧度信号只作为刷新与复用的二元开关，且无论刷新还是缓存都以固定步长推进，无法利用信号携带的更多信息，导致加速幅度受限且保真度下降。
   - 方法：提出SoftCache软缓存框架，核心观察是缓存刷新信号的“余量”（信号距阈值的距离）能预测复用所诱导的误差。余量大时采样器一次跳过多个调度节点，接近刷新时步长收缩回原始步长，允许步长落于调度网格之间使采样器脱离固定网格。方法无需训练、无需逐骨干网络调参，可直接用于图像与视频扩散/流模型。
   - 结果：在图像FLUX及视频HunyuanVideo、Wan2.1上，SoftCache在3.5倍至6.9倍加速区间内，于全部四项保真度指标上均领先所有基线，证明软缓存是一种通用、免训练且高保真的扩散加速方案，可显著降低迭代式生成的实际推理成本。

2. [Speculative Forcing for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22347v1)
   - 分类：Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：面向流式自回归视频生成的高效推理，token级提前接受机制对视频世界模型的低延迟部署价值大。
   - 痛点：少步自回归视频生成器虽支持流式生成，却对每个token都执行最终去噪步骤，无视其实际所需精炼程度：许多token在初始步就已接近最终全步输出，另一些则需精炼到结束，这种均匀计算造成大量冗余，限制了生成速度。
   - 方法：提出Speculative Forcing框架，将中间干净估计视为草稿，预测单个token何时可被安全提前接受。被接受的token冻结为最终输出、不再参与去噪，其缓存键值仍作为后续token的上下文。论文探索两种验证器：基于与干净内容相似度及跨步稳定性的免训练版本，以及读取冻结生成器中间特征来估计token距全步输出剩余差距的学习版本。
   - 结果：在短视频、长视频与世界生成三类任务上，Speculative Forcing在保持全步采样质量的同时大幅降低计算。LongLive上以约1.7个去噪步/token匹配四步生成器，加速超1.5倍，达41.7与37.8 fps；Lingbot-World 2.0相机运动持续改变可见内容时，以约2.5步/token维持质量并加速1.35倍至24.5 fps。

3. [Rethinking Routing Exploration in Modern Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22353v1)
   - 分类：MoE / Routing
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：直指现代MoE训练中的路由探索问题，对高效扩展大模型训练有普遍指导意义。
   - 痛点：现代MoE架构同时采用细粒度专家分割与共享专家：前者在固定激活率下增加专家组合以提升表达力，后者为每个token激活。二者形成探索困境——共享专家降低token表示变异性、使路由探索性下降，而细粒度分割因路由空间组合性放大反而更需探索，导致细粒度分割的额外表达力在实践中无法充分兑现。
   - 方法：提出MirrorMoE，通过token自适应地抑制不必要专家来收缩探索空间，从而调和上述探索困境。方法在更小的有效候选空间内引导路由更高效地探索，使细粒度专家分割的表达潜力得以释放，缓解共享专家带来的表示同质化对探索的抑制，且不改变标准MoE骨架。
   - 结果：在不同模型规模上，MirrorMoE均一致提升性能，验证了通过收缩探索空间来调和共享专家与细粒度分割冲突的有效性，为现代MoE训练中的路由探索问题提供了简单而通用的改进方向。

**主要痛点**
- 扩散与自回归生成中均匀计算造成大量冗余，推理成本主导生成开销，亟需免训练或轻量的加速机制。
- 现代MoE共享专家与细粒度分割在路由探索上相互冲突，导致表达力无法充分兑现。
- 长时程agent与长上下文任务中，KV缓存随时间增长、信用分配困难，训练与推理效率受限。

**方法趋势**
- 软缓存按刷新信号余量自适应调节采样步长，token合并依扩散动力学决定合并与保留。
- 投机提前接受冻结token；用视锥体板块做区域级上下文选择；token自适应抑制冗余专家。
- 将权重更新限制在最底奇异子空间、以集合级或窗口式RL优化后训练，兼顾保持性与目标性能。

**结果信号**
- SoftCache在FLUX与两个视频模型上实现3.5至6.9倍加速并全面领先保真度；Speculative Forcing以约1.7步/token匹配四步生成器。
- MirrorMoE在不同规模一致提升性能；Velox第48块注意力加速4.36倍且质量分差0.15内。
- SST保护99%子空间仍保持性能并将KL漂移降2至4倍；LangDiT创连续语言生成GenPPL新低。

### 具身智能 / 强化学习

- 论文数：86

**今日推荐精读 Top 3**

1. [Value as Memory: Online Value-Guided Post-training for General Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22368v1)
   - 分类：Robot RL / Online Post-training
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：直接对应具身智能与强化学习中的在线真机后训练，解决部分可观测与离线反馈滞后。
   - 痛点：通用机器人策略在真实世界后训练面临两个耦合痛点：部分可观测使动作选择存在歧义，机器人难以仅凭当前观测判断任务进度；离线后训练又让部署经验无法及时回流，策略改进滞后。长时程、可变形物体与接触丰富操作中，稀疏奖励使错误归因更难，已有离线RL或模仿学习难以融合在线自主探索，导致样本效率低、泛化到新场景能力弱。现有方法要么依赖大量演示，要么需要昂贵真机交互，难以在少量稀疏奖励下持续提升通用策略。
   - 方法：VaM将价值预测缓存为任务进度记忆，把近期价值估计作为动作条件，从而在部分可观测下显式保留历史进度信息。训练采用离线到在线的课程：先用离线示范做RL预热，再结合离策略干预与在策略rollout进行真机在线更新，使稀疏奖励下也能自主适应。系统还支持数据采集与训练并发，减少每梯度步墙钟时间，并让策略在部署中持续改进。价值缓存与在线更新解耦，既可复用已有示范，又能在新环境在线纠错。
   - 结果：在长时程可变形物体与接触丰富操作任务中，价值缓存带来平均绝对成功率提升29.4%，在线自主学习相对匹配的离线训练提升32.8%。相同梯度步预算下，在线调度完成约8倍策略迭代，每梯度步记录时间降低2倍以上，显示并发采集训练显著提高真机后训练效率。结果表明价值作为记忆能同时改善部分可观测下的动作选择与在线适应，为通用机器人策略从离线预热走向自主在线学习提供了可行路径。

2. [Spark-0: Scaling Spatially Aligned Tactile Pretraining for Robot Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22342v1)
   - 分类：Vision-Tactile-Language-Action / Dexterous Manipulation
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：触觉预训练用于灵巧操作，属于具身多模态策略与大规模预训练。
   - 痛点：通用机器人策略受益于大规模视觉预训练，但在接触丰富灵巧操作中，力、柔顺性与早期滑动难以从图像观测，纯视觉策略对接触状态不敏感。真实触觉数据规模小、跨本体迁移难，且触觉通常只作为部署时输入，未作为可扩展预训练监督。现有VTLA模型多依赖视觉-语言-动作，触觉通路常与视觉表示空间不对齐，导致触觉监督难以规模化。如何把触觉同时用于预训练监督和策略条件，并保持纯视觉部署兼容，是核心痛点。
   - 方法：Spark-0构建空间对齐的视觉-触觉-语言-动作模型。预训练阶段用触觉手套生成图像空间接触图，并用冻结3D几何教师特征监督视觉token，迫使视觉表示编码接触几何；同时触觉通路编码区域力历史与3D taxel状态，条件化动作专家。为规模化监督，作者构建1000小时全手触觉人类操作数据集HumanTouch，并联合第一视角视频、机器人数据与仿真预训练，单个checkpoint同时支持纯视觉与触觉条件部署。
   - 结果：在SparkArena两个赛道取得最高平均成功率，并在Ego Humanoid Manipulation Benchmark全部八项聚合指标排名第一。相比同数据同算力但丢弃触觉的纯视觉预训练，保留1000小时HumanTouch使Ego Humanoid视觉成功率从52.5%升至71.7%，SparkArena-Real成功率从44.0%升至52.0%。结果说明触觉不仅是有用的部署输入，也能作为可扩展监督提升视觉运动预训练，且不牺牲纯视觉部署能力。

3. [CoDA: Co-Evolution of Data Engines and Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22362v1)
   - 分类：Generative Simulation / Policy Co-evolution
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：面向机器人操作的数据引擎与策略闭环共进化，结合生成式仿真与RL/后训练。
   - 痛点：生成式仿真可自动构建机器人环境，但物理有效环境未必对训练有用；关键挑战是识别能暴露策略失败的环境，并生成帮助策略克服失败的监督。现有闭环数据生成常缺少对失败诊断、训练进度与监督信号的统一记忆，导致生成预算浪费、策略更新方向不明，仿真中表现好但OOD泛化差。如何让数据引擎与策略共同进化，是机器人学习数据飞轮的核心痛点。
   - 方法：CoDA让仿真数据引擎与动作策略通过共享异构记忆共进化，记忆记录环境构建、策略失败与学习进度。视觉语言critic给出诊断，系统把验证过的诊断编译为两类程序：重建困难的环境生成程序，以及帮助策略克服困难的训练程序。进一步提出Text2potential，将诊断转为分阶段势函数用于动作块价值学习，在标准塑形假设下保持任务最优策略，并用可测学习进度指导后续生成与程序精炼。
   - 结果：在四类操作任务族中，CoDA将监督微调后的OOD成功率从5.3%提升到27.6%，超过最强闭环基线9.5个百分点且生成预算相同。在LIBERO-Pro上，15轮生成与训练将位置扰动成功率从25%升至84%，指令扰动成功率从1%升至76%。结果表明失败驱动、记忆引导的数据引擎-策略共进化能显著提升闭环训练效率和OOD鲁棒性。

**主要痛点**
- 具身策略在真实长时程任务中面临部分可观测、接触状态不可见、环境因素不可观测与稀疏奖励，离线后训练反馈滞后。
- 生成式仿真数据虽多但未必有用，如何定位策略失败并生成针对性监督仍是闭环数据引擎的核心难题。
- 触觉、个性化偏好、记忆和世界模型预测等具身能力缺乏可扩展预训练与统一评估基准。

**方法趋势**
- 在线/离线混合RL、价值缓存、世界模型MPC与自博弈蒸馏用于真机策略后训练。
- 视觉-触觉-语言-动作预训练、空间对齐触觉监督和跨本体数据规模化。
- 失败诊断驱动的数据引擎-策略共进化、分阶段势函数塑形以及测试时环境隐变量适应。

**结果信号**
- VaM在长时程操作中取得29.4%至32.8%成功率提升并提升8倍策略迭代效率。
- Spark-0在灵巧操作基准取得领先，保留触觉使视觉成功率从52.5%升至71.7%。
- CoDA将OOD成功率从5.3%提升到27.6%，在LIBERO-Pro上将位置扰动成功率从25%升至84%。

### Agent

- 论文数：25

**今日推荐精读 Top 3**

1. [MLExpert: Towards a Generalist Agentic Model for Autonomous Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22344v1)
   - 分类：Agentic ML Engineering / MoE Post-training / RL
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：直接对应团队关注的自主智能体长时程训练、MoE 后训练与 AI4AI（递归自我改进）主线，可作为智能体 RL 训练配方与奖励设计的参考模板。
   - 痛点：长时程自主机器学习工程（MLE）中，一次完整运行包含大量昂贵的实验，而每个实验的反馈都混合了此前多个决策的影响，信用分配极其困难；现有 MLE 系统虽能由模型自主组织实验、依据执行反馈调整优化策略，但训练时难以从超长交互轨迹中筛选有效监督信号，也难以从中间结果而非最终成败中学习，导致自主优化能力受限、算力浪费严重。
   - 方法：作者提出面向长时程 MLE 优化的后训练方案并训练出 35B 的 MoE 智能体模型 MLExpert：先构建 Kaggle-MLE Factory 生成可执行任务与交互执行环境，采用层次化智能体 SFT，通过结果引导的监督把通用工具使用与编码能力迁移到 MLE；核心是窗口化交互强化学习 WIRL，把两次实验结果之间的多轮决策视为一个执行窗口并共享结果型奖励，同时用异步采集与策略校正支持在轨迹结束前就更新策略。
   - 结果：在 MLE-Bench Lite 上，MLExpert 把基座模型的平均奖牌率从 37.9% 提升到 72.7%，性能与 Kimi-K3、GLM-5.3 等更大的前沿模型相当；在 NatureBench Lite、智能体脚手架优化与算法优化任务上也取得提升，说明学到的优化能力可以迁移到其他任务，同时验证了窗口级结果奖励与异步策略更新对长时程智能体训练的有效性。

2. [Mosaic: Multi-Session Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22174v1)
   - 分类：Agentic RL / Multi-Session Training
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：直击团队智能体 RL 训练与推理形态不一致的问题（上下文压缩、subagent forking），其层次化优势分配思路可复用到我们现有 RL 管线。
   - 痛点：现有智能体强化学习方法大多假设轨迹在单一持续累积的上下文内完成，而推理时为了控制上下文又普遍使用上下文压缩与子智能体分叉，使一条轨迹实际跨越多个会话，训练假设与部署执行之间出现结构性错配；常见做法是把轨迹级优势广播到所有会话，既不区分各会话对结果的贡献，也无法引导轨迹内部的会话分配，训练信号粗糙且会话资源被大量浪费。
   - 方法：Mosaic 提出多会话智能体强化学习设定，允许一条轨迹包含任意数量会话，并采用无 critic 的层次化优化：轨迹层级把会话使用信号融入优势估计，用于刻画轨迹整体的完成质量；会话层级依据估计出的边际贡献对每个会话的优势进行重塑，从而区分不同会话对最终结果的贡献并引导会话分配；方法兼容上下文压缩与子智能体分叉两类执行范式，使训练形态与推理部署保持一致。
   - 结果：在 BrowseComp 上以 Qwen3.5-4B 为基座，Mosaic 在上下文压缩与子智能体分叉两种场景下都优于把轨迹优势广播到所有会话的基线，并且平均使用的会话数更少；分析显示方法同时改善了会话利用率与会话分配质量，即把训练信号更集中地分配给真正贡献结果的会话；作者据此认为，会话分配（而非仅上下文管理）是多会话智能体 RL 后续值得深入优化的目标。

3. [Librarian Agents: Training LLMs to Curate Long-Context Workspaces that Anticipate Future Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22273v1)
   - 分类：Long-Context Agents / Memory Curation / RL
   - 研究价值：值得精读
   - 关联：为智能体记忆与长上下文工作空间构建提供了可训练的范式，与团队在 agent memory、检索式长上下文与可复用工作区方向高度相关。
   - 痛点：长文档上下文推理仍是 LLM 的难点：标准检索方法本质上是反应式的，只能在推理时被动取用，复杂跨文档推理因此成为瓶颈；预计算结构如知识图谱又依赖刚性启发式，泛化性差。更关键的是，现代智能体框架中智能体擅长在已有文件系统里导航解决即时任务，却从未被训练去为未来查询构建可复用的工作空间，这种能力不对称使长上下文智能体在跨文档综合时既慢又不准。
   - 方法：Librarian Agents 让构建者智能体把原始文档转换为由合成文件与引用链接构成的层次化文件系统，为下游回答智能体定制可复用工作空间，从而把整理工作前移到推理之前；作者把工作空间构造形式化为马尔可夫决策过程，用强化学习优化构建策略，直接以下游回答准确率作为奖励信号，使整理行为面向未来多样化查询，而非只服务单一任务，并支持零样本迁移到未见语料。
   - 结果：在 BrowseComp-Plus 上，Librarian Agents 比最强的检索与预计算基线高出 33 个百分点，且一次构建的前置成本可在重复查询间摊销，查询越多单位成本越低；在 BRIGHT 的未见领域上零样本迁移带来平均 31 个百分点的准确率提升，表明学到的整理策略可跨域复用，为长上下文智能体的记忆与工作空间构建提供了新的训练范式。

**主要痛点**
- 长时程自主智能体的信用分配与训练-推理形态错配：多会话/多实验轨迹中难以筛选有效监督信号。
- 弱强模型协作与成本控制：何时升级、如何路由缺乏廉价可靠的不确定性或分歧信号。
- 智能体记忆与长上下文：写入、整理与评测仍与真实使用脱节，下游问答指标会掩盖记忆损坏。
- 多智能体协作的安全与合规：目标漂移、隐藏信念崩塌与策略违规缺乏可审计的评测手段。

**方法趋势**
- 面向智能体轨迹的强化学习与后训练：窗口化交互 RL、多会话层次化优势、块级 GRPO、分层 HTN 编排。
- 免训练或弱监督信号：隐状态潜在不确定性、模型间分歧、在线策略蒸馏与市场式投标自组织。
- 智能体系统与工具链：开放式 CUA 全栈、统一记忆写入模型、预判式工作空间整理、自动数据动作空间。
- 评测与审计：内在/外在记忆评测对比、多轮策略合规审计、心理支持量规、量表敏感性分析。

**结果信号**
- MLExpert 把 MLE-Bench Lite 平均奖牌率从 37.9% 提升到 72.7%，逼近更大前沿模型。
- Mosaic 在多会话智能体 RL 中优于轨迹级优势广播且会话更少；Librarian Agents 在 BrowseComp-Plus 领先 33 分、BRIGHT 零样本 +31 分。
- LEASH 在 OSWorld 上以 0.4117 美元/任务达到 73.66% 成功率，接近常开 Claude Opus 5 并省 19.2% 成本；OpenGCUA 在 OSWorld-v2 平均 +19.6 分、步数 -53%。
- 四智能体团队自主通关 Minecraft（2783 个程序、27 小时 14 分钟）；多智能体讨论中隐藏信念采信比例 51.6% 远高于公开表态 19.7%。

## Diffusion / 生成式AI

### 1. [Gen4P: Video Generators are 4D Perceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22371v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：gen4p, attention, video depth and camera, point tracking video depth, processes clean video latents, pretrained video generator
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22371v1>

**原始摘要**

We introduce Gen4P, a unified framework for recovering 4D geometry and motion from monocular video. We show that a pretrained video generator can be repurposed into a feed-forward 4D perceiver without iterative denoising. Building on on-demand querying, Gen4P processes clean video latents in a single encoder pass and predicts the requested 4D attributes through a lightweight cross-attention decoder. The on-demand query formulation enables flexible spatial sampling, and our proposed continuous-time queries further provide temporal flexibility. To strengthen geometric knowledge, we propose a straightforward plug-in solution by incorporating a pretrained video-depth LoRA as a frozen geometry prior, improving overall performance without adding extra trainable parameters. Gen4P achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on tasks of 3D point tracking, video depth and camera pose estimation. Qualitative results on in-the-wild videos illustrate its capability of tracking complex motion while maintaining the delicate structure. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of video generative pretraining for unified 4D perception. See https://gen4p.github.io for more visual results.

### 2. [Which Tokens to Merge? Diffusion Dynamics for Efficient Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22370v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diffusion, training-free strategy for accelerating, token merging, tokens that are BOTH, merged and which should, tokens into merging roles
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22370v1>

**原始摘要**

Token merging has emerged as an effective training-free strategy for accelerating diffusion models by reducing redundant token computation while minimizing generation degradation. A key challenge is determining which tokens can be safely merged and which should be preserved for subsequent computation. Existing method addresses this by preserving prompt-aligned foreground regions, thereby protecting semantically relevant structures. However, this text-relevance criterion overlooks background objects, local details that are not explicitly mentioned in the prompt, leading to degraded structural coherence. In this work, we propose Dynamics-aware Token Merging (DaTMe), a training-free framework that grounds token merging in the underlying diffusion dynamics. Our key insight is that token importance should reflect whether a token remains unresolved during denoising and whether it carries structural information. To this end, DaTMe constructs a dynamics- aware importance map from the Tweedie denoised estimate. The map combines a temporal signal, measuring inter-step changes in the predicted clean image, with a structural signal, capturing local high-frequency content such as boundaries and fine details. Using this map, DaTMe adaptively assigns tokens into merging roles under a given compression ratio: important tokens are preserved as anchors, while tokens that are both temporally stabilized and structurally homogeneous are selected as safe merge candidates. Experiments on PixArt-a and FLUX demonstrate that DaTMe maintains visual fidelity across the entire image, highlighting diffusion dynamics as an effective token-level merging decision in efficient generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/SeungJuCha/DaTMe.

### 3. [SoftCache: Adaptive Step Size Caching for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diffusion, generation cost. training-free caching, training-free caching accelerates THEM, cost. training-free caching accelerates, wan2.1 for video, FAR each cached STEP
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369v1>

**原始摘要**

Diffusion and flow models generate a sample over iterative steps, each a full pass through a large network, and that pass dominates generation cost. Training-free caching accelerates them by reusing activations from recent evaluations instead of recomputing them. Dynamic caching methods decide when to refresh by accumulating a cheap probe of the network input into a staleness signal and spending a fresh evaluation once it crosses a threshold. They perform hard caching: the signal is a binary switch between refresh and reuse, and every step, fresh or cached, advances by the prescribed step size. We observe that the signal carries much more than this switch. How far it sits below the threshold, its headroom, predicts the error induced by reuse. We propose SoftCache, which performs soft caching: instead of a binary switch, the headroom sets how far each cached step goes. Where the headroom is large, the sampler jumps past several scheduled nodes, and as a refresh nears, its steps shrink back toward the original step. These adaptive jumps may land between the scheduled nodes, taking the sampler off its fixed grid. SoftCache requires no training and adds no per-backbone settings: on FLUX for images and on HunyuanVideo and Wan2.1 for video, at speedups from $3.5\times$ to $6.9\times$, it is ahead of every baseline on all four fidelity metrics.

### 4. [T2-z: Test-Time Optimization of Environment Latents for Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22364v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：t2-z, environment latents, test-time optimization, robotic world, optimization stage, world models
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22364v1>

**原始摘要**

Predicting how the world responds to an action requires more than what can be seen: robots must account for environmental factors that are neither directly observable nor explicitly specified, from physical properties such as friction and mass to human-defined environmental rules. We introduce T2-Z for test-time optimization of environment latents, which augments a robotic world model with a learnable environment latent to condition world predictions. This compact latent captures underlying environmental variation without supervision of what environmental factors exist or what values they take. During training, T2-Z alternates between two stages: a world-model optimization stage that fixes the latents and learns general action-conditioned dynamics across environments; and an environment-latent optimization stage that freezes the world model and optimizes the conditioning latents, allowing latent structure to emerge purely through world prediction. At test time, the world model remains frozen and a newly initialized environment latent is optimized from one or more test-time interactions, adapting the model to previously unseen environmental configurations. Across 7 simulated settings and 4 real-world robotic tasks, T2-Z reduces object prediction error metric from 15.20 to 7.00 and raises action success from 33.73% to 72.96%. Its emergent latents capture structured representations of individual and compounded factors across diverse visual scenes. Ablations further show that T2-Z can efficiently infer environment latents from as little as a single test-time interaction trajectory.

### 5. [Perceive, Predict, and Plan: a Unified World Action Model for Egocentric Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22363v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：action model for egocentric, unified world action, egocentric dexterous, transformer, attention, diffusion
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22363v1>

**原始摘要**

We introduce P$^{3}$-WAM, a unified world action model for egocentric dexterous manipulation that learns 3D perception, action-conditioned prediction, and goal-directed planning through three complementary modes: Perceive, which reconstructs articulated hand motion and camera trajectories from video; Predict, which generates visual futures under prescribed actions; and Plan, which jointly generates action trajectories and their anticipated visual consequences conditioned on an initial image, a language instruction, and an optional visual goal. Our model integrates a compact, structured 3D action representation into a pretrained video diffusion transformer through a mixture-of-transformers architecture with shared base weights, modality-specific adaptation, and bidirectional visual-action attention. Randomized modality visibility lets video and 3D actions serve as either conditioning context or prediction targets across the three modes. Experiments show that unified training improves all three capabilities over single-mode training, including gains in planning quality under a vision-language model evaluation. Through unified training, our model outperforms specialized baselines on 3D hand reconstruction and action-conditioned video prediction across four datasets, while producing goal-directed manipulation videos with stronger instruction adherence and action progression than video-generation baselines. Project Page: https://p3-wam.pages.dev/

### 6. [Grounding Generative Verification in 3D Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark, reasoning, multimodal, large language models, analyze verification across fine-grained, verification across fine-grained factual
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360v1>

**原始摘要**

Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and 3D generation have expanded 3D understanding and synthesis. However, beyond visual fidelity, task-specific correctness grounded in 3D evidence remains a major challenge. We argue that building reliable 3D systems requires not only stronger generation and reasoning, but also verification feedback that identifies unmet requirements and explains their violations, allowing systems to reflect on and refine their outputs. Such feedback must integrate multi-view observations and distinguish genuine inconsistencies from projection-dependent appearances. We make three contributions. First, we introduce 3DVerifierBench, a benchmark spanning 10 tasks that evaluates decisions, rejection explanations, and responses to controlled changes in specifications and candidates. A human study reveals a 21.6 percentage-point gap between humans and the strongest tested model, GPT-6 Astra, while explanation-aware analysis exposes weaknesses in rejection reliability. Second, we analyze verification across fine-grained factual categories, exposing errors in values and target binding. We develop a scalable pipeline combining multi-view fact extraction, controlled 3D editing, and native annotations to produce 14,126 training examples with contradiction explanations and linked counterfactuals. Third, we examine how verification could support 3D generation through candidate selection and recursive refinement. In human-labeled sixteen-candidate pools, oracle selection improves complete factual success from 26.7% to 90.0%. In a separate eight-input study, human-guided recursive refinement satisfies all seven requirements for every input, compared with three under parallel Oracle@8. Together, our benchmark, analysis, and data pipeline establish a foundation for models to reflect on 3D content through reliable verification feedback.

### 7. [PathFM: Fast Generative Forecasting of Multivariate Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22358v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：pathfm achieves, attention, foundation models, established forecasting benchmarks fev-bench, forecasting model that USES, makes generative TIME series
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22358v1>

**原始摘要**

Time series foundation models are becoming increasingly popular for general-purpose forecasting. However, most leading models predict marginal quantiles, leaving the dependence structure of future trajectories unspecified. This distinction matters for trajectory-dependent quantities such as cumulative demand, peak load, and event timing, yet point and marginal scores do not reveal it. To model this dependence, we introduce PathFM, a trajectory-based forecasting model that uses flow matching to generate complete future paths. We further distill the model to one or two steps and share history attention cache across draws. Together, these enable PathFM to generate multiple paths for a single series in roughly the time needed for one query to a quantile model. The distilled sampler also makes it practical to optimize a calibration loss directly. PathFM achieves competitive marginal accuracy on the established forecasting benchmarks fev-bench and TIME. To complement these evaluations, we introduce Forecast Path Score Bench (fps-bench), which augments fev-bench tasks with energy and variogram scores and downstream metrics. PathFM achieves a higher aggregate path score on fps-bench than all evaluated external baselines. Experiments on chaotic systems further show that our flow model sustains long-run variability, while strong baselines rapidly collapse. PathFM makes generative time series forecasting both competitive and practical, enabling diverse downstream applications.

### 8. [OmniVerifier-C1: Decomposed Graded Verifier for Rule-Governed Generative World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22355v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：rule-governed generative world, reinforcement learning, graded verifier, multimodal, alignment, reasoning
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22355v1>

**原始摘要**

Generative visual verifiers play a crucial role in advancing multimodal intelligence. To enhance the robustness of visual verifiers in complex multimodal scenarios involving compositional requirements, we move beyond conventional binary verifiers that produce only True/False discrete judgments and introduce a verifier that automatically decomposes complex prompts or environmental rules into fine-grained components and assigns pointwise scores to provide a continuous and more informative critique signal. Our experiments further demonstrate that these structured and interpretable outputs improve the verifier's generalization to out-of-domain multimodal critic tasks. To train this verifier, we introduce a Decoupled RL training strategy that jointly optimizes sub-question-level binary judgment and global alignment scoring, using precise local feedback to develop fine-grained verification capabilities that provide a foundation for reliable continuous scoring at the global level. Under a general reinforcement learning framework, we first empirically demonstrate that the proposed pointwise graded verifier significantly outperforms binary verifiers in traditional text-to-image scenarios. We then extend our investigation to rule-governed generative world modeling where generative models are leveraged for world simulation and world reasoning under explicit rules. From a theoretical standpoint, we further show that a pairwise verification paradigm yields more accurate critiques than the pointwise formulation when ground-truth images are available, with empirical results on diverse structured world-modeling tasks such as Maze and Sudoku further validating the effectiveness of our approach. Overall, our findings suggest a key insight: the transition from generative models to structured world-modeling agents critically hinges on the availability of accurate pairwise graded visual verifiers.

### 9. [SUPRA-TEX: 3D Generation with Super-Aligned Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22352v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：alignment, transformer, datasets supra-tex achieves SOTA, public datasets supra-tex achieves, super-aligned texture. single image, end-to-end asset generation matching
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22352v1>

**原始摘要**

Single image 3D generation aims to dress a given 3D mesh with relightable materials while faithfully preserving the visual details of a reference image. Existing methods either introduce artifacts when fusing inconsistent multi-view predictions or struggle to preserve fine details through implicit image-feature conditioning. To resolve these challenges, we introduce SUPRA-Tex, built on the principle of decoupling visible-material estimation, surface alignment, and observation-guided 3D completion. This division of labor allows the 3D generator, trained on limited 3D data, to focus its capacity on preserving visible evidence and completing unobserved surfaces. Specifically, we first train an MLLM-based image editing model to predict lighting-separated PBR maps in the reference view. We then project these maps onto the visible surface and encode them with the pretrained material VAE encoder into native observation latents that share the generation target's latent space. An interleaved flow transformer holds these latents as fixed, read-only anchors while alternating unobserved-region and full-surface updates to generate complete material latents. This design achieves super-alignment with the reference, delivering What You See Is What You Get fidelity on visible regions and coherent completion on unobserved surfaces. Trained solely on public datasets, SUPRA-Tex achieves SOTA performance in both geometry-conditioned texturing and end-to-end asset generation, matching or even exceeding leading commercial systems on in-the-wild images. Project page: https://supra-3d.github.io/supratex/

### 10. [Speculative Forcing for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22347v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：speculative forcing, autoregressive video, video generation, denoising steps PER token, long-video and world generation, generation. few-step autoregressive
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22347v1>

**原始摘要**

Few-step autoregressive video generators enable streaming generation, yet they run every token through the final denoising step, regardless of how much refinement it needs. We find this uniform computation is often unnecessary: many tokens already closely match their eventual full-step outputs in the initial steps, while others require refinement until the end. We introduce Speculative Forcing, a framework that treats intermediate clean estimates as drafts and predicts when individual tokens can be safely accepted early. Once accepted, a token is frozen as its final output and undergoes no further denoising, while its cached keys and values remain available as context for the remaining tokens. We explore two alternative verifier designs: a training-free one based on similarity to clean content and stability across steps, and a learned one that reads the frozen generator's intermediate features to estimate each token's remaining gap to its full-step output. Across short-video, long-video, and world generation, Speculative Forcing preserves the quality of full-step sampling while substantially reducing computation. On LongLive, it matches the four-step generator with ~1.7 denoising steps per token, running over 1.5x faster at 41.7 and 37.8 fps. On Lingbot-World 2.0, where camera motion continually changes the visible content, it maintains quality with ~2.5 denoising steps per token and runs 1.35x faster at 24.5 fps.

### 11. [GeoState: Learning View-Decoupled Geometry States for 3D World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22343v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：view-decoupled geometry, world models, alignment, autoregressive, foundation models, modeling that separates scene-state
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22343v1>

**原始摘要**

Recent 3D world models increasingly use features from geometry foundation models (GFMs) as predictive world states, benefiting from their rich geometric priors. However, the view dependence of these features entangles scene dynamics with camera-induced variation, making the resulting state evolution harder to predict. We propose GeoState, a view-decoupled geometry representation for predictive 3D world modeling that separates scene-state evolution from camera-dependent observation. Specifically, a scene tokenizer aggregates multi-view GFM features into a view-decoupled scene state, which a geometry decoder queries using camera rays. An autoregressive world model then predicts future scene states, while camera motion is modeled separately. The predicted states can therefore be decoded under either predicted or externally specified cameras, while retaining a single context-derived scale throughout the rollout without per-frame scale alignment. Experiments show that GeoState outperforms prior 3D world models on Waymo, supports geometrically consistent cross-view querying, and generalizes zero-shot to KITTI and nuScenes. Project page: https://hongchang-liu.github.io/GeoState-project-page/

### 12. [Dexterity from Supervised Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22340v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：dexterity from supervised, reinforcement learning, PLAY policy, supervised PLAY, world model., inference the SAME PLAY
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22340v1>

**原始摘要**

How can robots autonomously collect the contact-rich experience needed to learn dexterous manipulation? We present DSP: Dexterity from supervised play, a framework in which a robot learns a play policy from minutes of human play through behavior cloning, then deploys it autonomously to collect hours of interaction data to train a world model. During inference, the same play policy then guides model-predictive control (MPC) by proposing candidate action sequences for evaluation under the learned world model. To accelerate inference, we also introduce a method for distilling a closed-loop, goal-conditioned policy through self-play within the learned world model. We evaluate this framework on underactuated pushing and any-to-any in-hand pose reorientation with a multifingered hand, in simulation and real world. In real world experiment, our method outperforms hindsight behavior cloning by 69.6% in success rate and achieves comparable success to reinforcement learning with 490 times less interaction data. These results demonstrate that supervised play can autonomously collect interaction data, guide model-based planning, and enable policy learning without any task-specific demonstrations or sim-to-real transfer.

### 13. [Illumination-Aware Active Perception for Spacecraft Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22335v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：spacecraft inspection., line-of-sight inspection metrics, illumination-aware active perception, BAND and for purely, compared with for geometric, spacecraft materials and TEND
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22335v1>

**原始摘要**

Harsh and rapidly varying orbital illumination introduces failure modes during spacecraft inspection. Existing planners rely on line-of-sight inspection metrics which perform occlusion checks against a known target and conflate geometric visibility with task-relevant information. These metrics overlook illumination effects including eclipse, self-shadowing, and specular reflection from spacecraft materials and tend to declare components as inspected despite the corresponding images lacking sufficient information. We propose an inspection-planning framework that replaces geometric visibility with a learned measure of camera information derived from a classifier variational autoencoder (VAE) that operates directly on the captured image. The encoder model is trained to classify spacecraft surface components from rendered patches, and its total latent variance is used to learn the varying illumination of said components, replacing binary visibility checks with a continuous, image-grounded quantity. We evaluate the approach by implementing a Guidance, Navigation, and Control architecture in a photorealistic physics simulator, and vary lighting and viewing geometries with multiple orbits. We show that, although all approaches successfully maximize their own objective, our approach results in better illumination: a mean brightness of $0.57$, with $81.6\%$ of observations above $0.25$, compared with $0.02$ ($3.0\%$) for geometric planning with an illumination band and $0.04$ ($6.0\%$) for purely geometric planning.

### 14. [Designing Transformers for Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22330v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：continuous diffusion language, transformer, diffusion language models., language generation, large language models, NOT optimized for cdlms.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22330v1>

**原始摘要**

Continuous diffusion language models (CDLMs) offer an alternative approach to parallel language generation; however, most existing studies directly use transformer architectures from either causal large language models (LLMs) or Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for image generation, which were not optimized for CDLMs. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study on Transformer architectures for CDLMs and introduce Language Diffusion Transformer (LangDiT), combining feature and query-key normalization, wider noise conditioning, likelihood-shortcut gradient routing and calibration, and confidence-gated training self-conditioning. LangDiT significantly reduces generation perplexity in matched LangFlow comparisons, establishing a new state of the art in continuous language generation across different model formulations. Using LangFlow as a representative CDLM, LangDiT as a plug-and-play backbone leads to a new state-of-the-art model with a generation perplexity (GenPPL) of 21.72 using 1024 NFEs and 44.72 GenPPL using 128 NFEs on the unconditional generation of 1024-token sequences on the OpenWebText dataset. When adapted to Plaid-style CDLM formulations, LangDiT results in a new leading GenPPL of 11.33 using 1024 denoising NFEs, and 27.51 GenPPL using 128 denoising NFEs. These gains are achieved while retaining each model's underlying representation, training objective, and inference sampler, isolating transformer architecture as a fundamental source of improvement. Our study identifies architectural design as an important source of improvement in continuous diffusion language models.

### 15. [AVC-Reward: Event-Centric Audio-Video Consistency Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22321v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：audio-video consistency, audio-video generation, alignment, benchmark, general formulation for audio-video, trainable audio-video reward model.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22321v1>

**原始摘要**

As joint audio-video generation models become more capable, they can generate audio-visual scenes exhibiting three forms of complexity: multi-event composition, multi-layer audio mixture, and asymmetric cross-modal relations. However, current evaluations largely reuse metrics from earlier simple and unidirectional tasks. They typically evaluate an audio-video clip as a whole and rely on simple predefined relations, making them unsuitable for these increasingly complex scenes. We propose an event-centric evaluation framework that disentangles audio and visual events at the event level, analyzes each event independently, cross-validates relations across modalities, and aggregates the judgments into a coarse-to-fine, three-level consistency metric. The metric assesses global narrative harmony, event-level temporal synchronization, and fine-grained attribute alignment, providing a general formulation for audio-video consistency evaluation across diverse generation settings and cross-modal relations. We instantiate the metric as AVC-Reward, a trainable audio-video reward model. To evaluate the agreement between automatic judgments and human perception across generation paradigms and relation complexities, we construct AVC-Benchmark, which is primarily built from diverse positive and negative samples generated by various types of audio-video generation models. AVC-Reward achieves state-of-the-art alignment with human judgment among automatic methods, provides interpretable rationales that localize failures, and improves the audio-video consistency of a joint generation model when used as an online reinforcement-learning signal. Project page: https://tsuki0512.github.io/AVC-Reward.github.io/

### 16. [Not Just a Subject: Capturing Adaptive Scene-Specific Variance for Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22320v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark, attention, diffusion, text-to-image, scene-specific variance for visual, scene-specific adaptation across TEXT
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22320v1>

**原始摘要**

Recent advancements in diffusion models have significantly improved text-to-image generation, enabling their application to visual storytelling, where coherent image sequences are generated from narrative prompts. However, existing methods primarily focus on identity preservation and often fail to adapt to diverse scene variations, resulting in semantic misalignment and structural inflexibility. We propose EOS-Story, a training-free framework that harmonizes identity consistency with scene-specific adaptation across text and vision spaces. In the text space, we decouple the narrative into identity and scene sub-prompts to prevent information dilution, but this leaves the scene without access to appearance cues. To bridge this gap, we introduce Cross-Contextual Bridging, which softly injects a phrase-level appearance residual into the scene's <EOS> embedding. In the vision space, we find that scale mismatch between identity and scene subjects causes a few identity tokens to be repeatedly transferred, producing structural artifacts. We address this with Adaptive Identity Preservation, which anchors identity features at the scene-specific scale (Identity Anchoring) and integrates them via Identity-Aware Self-Attention. To evaluate performance under varied spatial compositions, we extend the Consistory+ benchmark with diverse framing scenarios (Consistory+_+). Extensive experiments show that EOS-Story achieves competitive or superior performance on both benchmarks.

### 17. [Velox: Spatial Memory Makes World Models Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22311v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：attention, world models, diffusion, velox-world.com velox for MORE, frustum SLAB, https velox-world.com velox
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22311v1>

**原始摘要**

Video world models turn actions and camera motion into long visual rollouts, making video a natural interface for interactive simulation. Such interaction requires responsive generation, yet coherent rollouts must retain visual memory of what has already been generated. Chunked video diffusion models usually keep this context in growing KV caches, so later chunks become progressively slower. To address this bottleneck, we introduce Velox, a training-free attention router for region-level context selection using frustum slab correspondences from streaming geometry. Velox represents historical blocks as camera frustum slabs with depth intervals and obtains current-block depth through recency-priority multiwarp. For well-covered regions, frustum slab overlap softly modulates each layer's model-derived block importance. Uncovered regions retain the model-derived route without geometric bias. The fixed token-block granularity matches FlashAttention-style tiled execution and reduces the attention term that grows with rollout length. Frustum slab construction runs asynchronously with denoising on the same H200. At chunk 48, Velox accelerates attention by $4.36\times$ and denoising by $2.43\times$. On the full 158-case WBench navigation split, it remains within 0.15 API-free average points of dense LingBot-Fast-Cam. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/velox/ for more details.

### 18. [Multi-Target Few-Shot Prompting with Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22294v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：few-shot prompting, prompting with diffusion language, multi-target few-shot, autoregressive, diffusion language models. few-shot, example-supported infills. across few-shot
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22294v1>

**原始摘要**

Few-shot prompting is useful when instructions and rewards are hard to specify. One such application is editing by example, where a demonstrated formatting change or API migration may need to be applied across multiple sites. However, such edits often depend on both left and right context, making generation with causal autoregressive models awkward. We therefore introduce multi-target few-shot prompting with diffusion language models, where examples guide the infilling of multiple masked regions within a shared canvas. To address the known challenge of reliably following demonstrations in few-shot prompting, we further leverage selective masking of the canvas to derive a likelihood-ratio reward that favors example-supported infills. Across few-shot prompting benchmarks and software refactoring case studies, we find that demonstrations improve infilling accuracy, with example guidance yielding further gains in several settings.

### 19. [Routing Policy Optimization for Online Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22282v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, attention, routing policy optimization, diffusion, text-to-image, text-to-video
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22282v1>

**原始摘要**

Reinforcement learning can align visual generators with reward signals beyond their pretraining objectives, but existing score-function methods typically introduce stochasticity by perturbing the reverse denoising trajectory, leading to costly rollout-based optimization. We instead ask whether stochastic exploration can be introduced inside the generator while preserving its original sampler. Routing Policy Optimization (RPO) answers this question by turning attention routing into a stochastic exploration action. At a few selected denoising levels, each query block keeps its own block and samples further key blocks in proportion to the generator's native attention mass, or keeps dense attention. The sampled route perturbs the velocity of that transition, and a Gaussian likelihood around the dense velocity scores the perturbation with a score-function objective. RPO further uses a controlled two-rollout estimator: two rollouts share the same prompt, initial latent, routed denoising levels, and dense anchor layers, differing only in their sampled attention routes. Their reward difference provides an advantage signal for the routed transitions. Because the likelihood differentiates the dense velocity itself, the learned updates remain effective after routing is removed. At inference, RPO restores dense attention and retains the original deterministic sampler. Experiments on text-to-image and text-to-video generation show that RPO improves generation quality while achieving nearly 4x higher training efficiency than Flow-GRPO-style methods. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/rpo for visual results.

### 20. [Beyond Pairwise Repulsion: Collective Diversity via Determinant Maximization in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22267v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diffusion, text-to-image, sampling toward high-density modes, achieves leading intra-batch diversity, concentrates sampling toward high-density, noise-optimization and repulsion objectives
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22267v1>

**原始摘要**

Strong guidance in text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models concentrates sampling toward high-density modes of the conditional distribution, collapsing independently sampled noise vectors onto nearly identical visual prototypes. This limits utility for tasks that demand distributional breadth, most critically synthetic data augmentation. Diversity, however, is a collective property of the whole batch: a set can preserve every pairwise distance yet still collapse onto a low-dimensional subspace. Existing sum-of-pairwise noise-optimization and repulsion objectives respond only weakly to this higher-order failure. We propose DetNO (Determinant Noise Optimization), which formulates diverse generation as RKHS volume maximization on the initial noise, maximizing the log-determinant of an RBF Gram matrix over one-step Tweedie predictions. This set-level functional equals the squared batch volume; its repulsion is strongest when the batch is most collapsed and self-attenuates as diversity is achieved. An anchor-based fidelity term keeps the optimized noises near plausible regions, and Lagrangian relaxation gives a two-term objective with a single hyperparameter $λ$ governing the diversity-quality trade-off, without training or sampler modification. Across three backbones (SD1.5, SDXL, SD3), DetNO achieves leading intra-batch diversity in VendiScore and Mean Self-Similarity, and a downstream few-shot classification experiment confirms that broader mode coverage brings statistically significant accuracy gains for synthetic data augmentation in the low-shot regime. Fixing the pipeline and varying only the batch objective, both RBF-based aggregators outperform InfoNCE, and the log-determinant attains the best downstream accuracy on both benchmarks. Its diversity advantage over InfoNCE widens as the batch grows. Code is available at https://github.com/mobled37/DetNO.

### 21. [SCOPE: Semantic Bounded Credit Optimization for Prompt Engineering in Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22264v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, prompt engineering, text-to-image, sd3 and flux.1-dev scope, flux.1-dev scope achieves average, prompt and its semantic
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22264v1>

**原始摘要**

Reinforcement learning (RL) enables language models to improve text-to-image (T2I) generation by rewriting prompts for a frozen image generator. Existing methods typically apply a single signal derived from the image to individual subword tokens, although objects, counts, attributes, and relations form coherent semantic spans. We propose SCOPE, an objective based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that aligns update weighting with this structure. The rewriter jointly generates an enhanced prompt and its semantic partition. SCOPE pools shifts in policy log probabilities within each span to derive a shared coefficient for its token losses while retaining a single advantage for each completion. It requires neither separate span rewards nor additional image renders. Across Qwen-Image, SD3, and FLUX.1-dev, SCOPE achieves average relative gains over vanilla GRPO of 9.48% on GeneVal and 7.62% on T2I-CompBench. Ablation studies show that both semantic boundaries and pooling within spans contribute to these gains, supporting semantic structure as a practical inductive bias for prompt engineering with RL.

### 22. [Topology-Inspired Fine-Tuning for Wind-Field Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22262v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-tuning, benchmark, CNN and GAN wind-field, CNN with differentiable topology-inspired, differentiable topology-inspired auxiliary losses, wind-field super-resolution. scientific super-resolution
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22262v1>

**原始摘要**

Scientific super-resolution (SR) models are typically optimized with pointwise reconstruction losses, yet scientific interpretation often hinges on extrema, connected regions, and multiscale scalar structure. We characterize this tension with pretrained CNN and GAN wind-field SR models. Pointwise metrics, bottleneck persistence-diagram (PD) distance, and merge-tree (MT) distance favor the CNN, whereas order-2 Wasserstein PD distances favor the sharper GAN, so even different distances on the same topological descriptor reward different properties. Motivated by this disagreement, we fine-tune the pretrained CNN with differentiable topology-inspired auxiliary losses on scalar speed, gradient magnitude, soft high-speed level sets, and local maxima, and with fixed-pair supervision, which constrains predicted values and persistence at ground-truth persistence-pair endpoints located offline with the Topology ToolKit. A loss study of 16 fine-tuning configurations, each compared with a matched standard fine-tuning control that uses only the original reconstruction loss, on a fixed 168-field benchmark (with topology evaluated on a fixed 160 by 160 subregion of each field), shows that, among the scalar-field losses, gradient supervision provides the strongest PD improvement but does not improve mean MT distance, whereas fixed-pair supervision gives the largest and most consistent joint improvement in PD and MT distances. The final objective combines these two signals. Relative to the pretrained CNN, it lowers mean bottleneck, $W_{2,\infty}$, and $W_{2,2}$ distances by 31%, 25%, and 27% (and by 32-35% relative to the control, which on its own worsens all three), lowers all three on 150 of 168 fields, and gains 1.30 dB PSNR, about half the gain of standard fine-tuning. Mean MT distance also improves, but on only 91 of those 150 fields, indicating that PD and MT capture complementary structure.

### 23. [Denoising Textures is a Different Ballgame Altogether!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22258v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-tuning, altogether the human brain, image denoising the problem, textures from visual stimuli, ballgame altogether the human, deciphers colors and textures
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22258v1>

**原始摘要**

The human brain effortlessly deciphers colors and textures from visual stimuli and reconstructs the world around us, yet preserving such details remains challenging in image denoising. While significant progress has been made in generic image denoising, the problem of texture denoising has remained relatively under-explored. Fine details, such as intricate patterns and repetitive structures, often suffer from over-smoothing even in deep learning based approaches for denoising. In this paper, we analyze the behavior of state-of-the-art (SOTA) denoisers on texture datasets and show that performance improvements are possible only by fine-tuning the denoisers on the texture datasets. Surprisingly, fine-tuning on texture datasets result in the models catastrophically forgetting the original dataset. These findings highlight the need for specialized treatment of textures in denoising. Since denoising quality directly impacts downstream tasks such as generative modelling, we argue that dedicated texture aware denoisers are essential for faithful texture reconstruction and synthesis.

### 24. [Query-Efficient Rejection Sampling for Parallel Masked Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22238v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：rejection sampling, diffusion, single-increment rejection sampler SIRS, BUT sampling these tokens, sampling decomposing the LOG, sampling for parallel masked
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22238v1>

**原始摘要**

Masked diffusion models (MDMs) accelerate generation by unmasking multiple tokens in parallel, but sampling these tokens independently ignores their correlations and biases the output. Anari et al. (2026) corrected this bias with rejection sampling, decomposing the log acceptance probability into a sum of token-level terms, but the evaluation requires one score query per unmasked token. We propose the Single-Increment Rejection Sampler (SIRS), which provides an estimate of the log acceptance probability from a single randomly sampled increment at the cost of one query. SIRS then converts a Poisson number of clipped estimates into accept/reject decisions through a Bernoulli factory adapted from Chen et al. (2026). Since each query returns the conditionals of all remaining tokens in the block, we further Rao-Blackwellize each increment by averaging over these tokens at no extra cost, thereby reducing clipping. We show that the KL-divergence between the data distribution and the SIRS output is bounded by a clipping term plus the model's training error. With exact scores, parallel decoding pays the full within-block dependence, whereas SIRS pays only the part beyond the clipping range.

### 25. [MOSS: Mode-Oriented Source Shaping for Robot Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22193v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：mode-oriented source shaping, FLOW matching, multimodal, diffusion, real-world manipulation tasks MOSS, FLOW transport induces mode-dependent
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22193v1>

**原始摘要**

In robotic manipulation, the same observation often admits multiple feasible actions. Generative policies such as diffusion and flow matching naturally capture such multimodal behaviors, yet which mode is executed is left to random sampling and cannot be actively controlled. We propose Mode-Oriented Source Shaping (MOSS), which turns mode selection into an explicit choice of where generation starts. We first reveal, both empirically and theoretically, that deterministic flow transport induces mode-dependent structure in the source space. Under ideal deterministic ODE transport, distinct behavioral modes have distinct source-space preimages, while practical approximation errors can blur their boundaries due to imbalanced data, limited model capacity, and few inference steps. Motivated by this source-space structure, MOSS constructs semantically aligned mode-specific Gaussian sources that encode mode identity in the initial noise. A modality-strength estimator predicts the local degree of multimodality, which adaptively controls the geometry of the mode-specific sources. It contracts them toward a shared Gaussian where behaviors are similar and separates them near behavioral branching points. During deployment, the desired behavior is selected at inference simply by sampling from the corresponding component. Across 6 simulated and 3 real-world manipulation tasks, MOSS steers to the target mode up to 100% of the time with high task success, recovers minority modes at over 90% guidance rate under a 10:90 imbalance dataset, and surpasses mode-conditioned flow policy by 43.3 points of guidance rate on real robots.

## 高效训练与推理

### 1. [LIBERO-PeRM: Benchmarking Personalized Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22372v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：personalized robotic, robotic manipulation, benchmark, agents. libero-perm studies FIVE, libero-perm studies FIVE questions, embodied agents. libero-perm studies
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22372v1>

**原始摘要**

Personalization is essential for general-purpose robots to transition into household environments and achieve true practicality. While general robotic manipulation has been extensively studied, personalized robotic manipulation (PeRM) lacks a controlled benchmark for separating task completion from preference satisfaction. We introduce LIBERO-PeRM, a benchmark for preference learning in embodied agents. LIBERO-PeRM studies five questions: 1) how policies follow preferences in zero-shot settings; 2) how they learn single preferences from demonstrations; 3) how efficiently they acquire preferences as demonstrations increase; 4) how learned preferences generalize across layouts and tasks; and 5) how policies compose preferences and behave under conflicts. We propose a controlled formulation and taxonomy of observable preferences in simulation, covering motion, skill, and user-profile choices. This formulation is restricted to language-expressible preferences that can be instantiated in LIBERO without changing the task goal, evaluated automatically, and reused across tasks. Building on LIBERO, our procedural pipeline generates paired demonstrations with and without preferences. We create five task suites (312 tasks) with high-quality demonstrations, rich metadata, and preference-satisfaction metrics. Experiments show that current VLA policies struggle with zero-shot personalization, while SFT makes preferences learnable. Mixed training achieves better no-preference retention than sequential adaptation and improves performance with contextual prompts compared with preference-only training, but can weaken motion-preference learning; preference generalization, decomposition, and conflict handling remain difficult. The code, datasets, and demos can be found on the project website: https://libero-perm.github.io/.

### 2. [Gen4P: Video Generators are 4D Perceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22371v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：gen4p, attention, video depth and camera, point tracking video depth, processes clean video latents, pretrained video generator
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22371v1>

**原始摘要**

We introduce Gen4P, a unified framework for recovering 4D geometry and motion from monocular video. We show that a pretrained video generator can be repurposed into a feed-forward 4D perceiver without iterative denoising. Building on on-demand querying, Gen4P processes clean video latents in a single encoder pass and predicts the requested 4D attributes through a lightweight cross-attention decoder. The on-demand query formulation enables flexible spatial sampling, and our proposed continuous-time queries further provide temporal flexibility. To strengthen geometric knowledge, we propose a straightforward plug-in solution by incorporating a pretrained video-depth LoRA as a frozen geometry prior, improving overall performance without adding extra trainable parameters. Gen4P achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on tasks of 3D point tracking, video depth and camera pose estimation. Qualitative results on in-the-wild videos illustrate its capability of tracking complex motion while maintaining the delicate structure. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of video generative pretraining for unified 4D perception. See https://gen4p.github.io for more visual results.

### 3. [Which Tokens to Merge? Diffusion Dynamics for Efficient Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22370v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diffusion, training-free strategy for accelerating, token merging, tokens that are BOTH, merged and which should, tokens into merging roles
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22370v1>

**原始摘要**

Token merging has emerged as an effective training-free strategy for accelerating diffusion models by reducing redundant token computation while minimizing generation degradation. A key challenge is determining which tokens can be safely merged and which should be preserved for subsequent computation. Existing method addresses this by preserving prompt-aligned foreground regions, thereby protecting semantically relevant structures. However, this text-relevance criterion overlooks background objects, local details that are not explicitly mentioned in the prompt, leading to degraded structural coherence. In this work, we propose Dynamics-aware Token Merging (DaTMe), a training-free framework that grounds token merging in the underlying diffusion dynamics. Our key insight is that token importance should reflect whether a token remains unresolved during denoising and whether it carries structural information. To this end, DaTMe constructs a dynamics- aware importance map from the Tweedie denoised estimate. The map combines a temporal signal, measuring inter-step changes in the predicted clean image, with a structural signal, capturing local high-frequency content such as boundaries and fine details. Using this map, DaTMe adaptively assigns tokens into merging roles under a given compression ratio: important tokens are preserved as anchors, while tokens that are both temporally stabilized and structurally homogeneous are selected as safe merge candidates. Experiments on PixArt-a and FLUX demonstrate that DaTMe maintains visual fidelity across the entire image, highlighting diffusion dynamics as an effective token-level merging decision in efficient generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/SeungJuCha/DaTMe.

### 4. [SoftCache: Adaptive Step Size Caching for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diffusion, generation cost. training-free caching, training-free caching accelerates THEM, cost. training-free caching accelerates, wan2.1 for video, FAR each cached STEP
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369v1>

**原始摘要**

Diffusion and flow models generate a sample over iterative steps, each a full pass through a large network, and that pass dominates generation cost. Training-free caching accelerates them by reusing activations from recent evaluations instead of recomputing them. Dynamic caching methods decide when to refresh by accumulating a cheap probe of the network input into a staleness signal and spending a fresh evaluation once it crosses a threshold. They perform hard caching: the signal is a binary switch between refresh and reuse, and every step, fresh or cached, advances by the prescribed step size. We observe that the signal carries much more than this switch. How far it sits below the threshold, its headroom, predicts the error induced by reuse. We propose SoftCache, which performs soft caching: instead of a binary switch, the headroom sets how far each cached step goes. Where the headroom is large, the sampler jumps past several scheduled nodes, and as a refresh nears, its steps shrink back toward the original step. These adaptive jumps may land between the scheduled nodes, taking the sampler off its fixed grid. SoftCache requires no training and adds no per-backbone settings: on FLUX for images and on HunyuanVideo and Wan2.1 for video, at speedups from $3.5\times$ to $6.9\times$, it is ahead of every baseline on all four fidelity metrics.

### 5. [Value as Memory: Online Value-Guided Post-training for General Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22368v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：value caching, online autonomous learning, reinforcement learning, caching and online real-world, post-training for general robot, task-progress memory while online
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22368v1>

**原始摘要**

Real-world post-training of generalist robot policies faces two challenges: partial observability makes action selection ambiguous, while offline post-training methods delay feedback from deployment experience. We present Value as Memory (VaM), a framework that addresses these challenges through value caching and online real-world reinforcement learning (RL). Value caching conditions actions on recent value predictions as task-progress memory, while online updates enable timely policy improvement by combining offline demonstrations, off-policy interventions, and on-policy rollouts for sample-efficient adaptation. As a result, VaM transitions from offline RL warmup to online autonomous learning with sparse rewards. Across long-horizon tasks involving deformable objects and contact-rich manipulation, VaM yields a mean absolute success-rate gain of $29.4\%$ with value caching, while achieving a $32.8\%$ gain with online autonomous learning over matched offline training. The evaluated online schedule performs $8\times$ as many policy iterations under the same gradient-step budget, with more than $2\times$ lower recorded time per gradient step with concurrent data collection and training.

### 6. [Discovering state-of-the-art quantum circuits with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22367v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：large language models, large-scale quantum information processors, discovering state-of-the-art quantum circuits, BUT realizing useful quantum, circuits with large language, quantum simulation and number
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22367v1>

**原始摘要**

Significant efforts are currently being directed toward the implementation of large-scale quantum information processors, but realizing useful quantum advantage also requires the development of efficient quantum algorithms. While for many practical problems the dominant resource costs are associated with a small set of computational primitives, optimizing them in the quantum setting presents unique challenges, including a reversibility constraint absent from ordinary classical computation. Here we introduce an evolutionary framework in which large language models (LLMs) propose circuit designs and an independent evaluator verifies and scores them. We apply this framework to tasks spanning numerical computation, chemistry, optimization, quantum simulation, and number theory. In seven of the eight tasks, the discovered circuits significantly reduce both Toffoli and qubit counts relative to the state of the art. In the eighth, the search discovers a new reversible architecture for prime-field modular inversion. By handling reversibility differently, the architecture makes divsteps -- a classical algorithm previously impractical for reversible integer arithmetic -- viable and substantially improves the Toffoli-qubit trade-off for modular inversion. These results open the door for evaluator-guided LLM co-design of new algorithmic structures for quantum computation.

### 7. [T2-z: Test-Time Optimization of Environment Latents for Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22364v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：t2-z, environment latents, test-time optimization, robotic world, optimization stage, world models
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22364v1>

**原始摘要**

Predicting how the world responds to an action requires more than what can be seen: robots must account for environmental factors that are neither directly observable nor explicitly specified, from physical properties such as friction and mass to human-defined environmental rules. We introduce T2-Z for test-time optimization of environment latents, which augments a robotic world model with a learnable environment latent to condition world predictions. This compact latent captures underlying environmental variation without supervision of what environmental factors exist or what values they take. During training, T2-Z alternates between two stages: a world-model optimization stage that fixes the latents and learns general action-conditioned dynamics across environments; and an environment-latent optimization stage that freezes the world model and optimizes the conditioning latents, allowing latent structure to emerge purely through world prediction. At test time, the world model remains frozen and a newly initialized environment latent is optimized from one or more test-time interactions, adapting the model to previously unseen environmental configurations. Across 7 simulated settings and 4 real-world robotic tasks, T2-Z reduces object prediction error metric from 15.20 to 7.00 and raises action success from 33.73% to 72.96%. Its emergent latents capture structured representations of individual and compounded factors across diverse visual scenes. Ablations further show that T2-Z can efficiently infer environment latents from as little as a single test-time interaction trajectory.

### 8. [CoDA: Co-Evolution of Data Engines and Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22362v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-tuning, vision-language, action policy co-evolve through, CODA improves out-of-distribution success, families CODA improves out-of-distribution, engines and action policies.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22362v1>

**原始摘要**

Generative simulation can automate robot environment construction, but physically valid environments are not necessarily useful for training. The challenge is to identify environments that address a policy's failures and supervision that helps it overcome them. We introduce CoDA, a framework in which a simulation data engine and an action policy co-evolve through a shared heterogeneous memory of environment construction, policy failures, and learning progress. The memory compiles validated diagnoses from a vision-language critic into generation programs that recreate difficulties and training programs that help the policy overcome them. We further propose Text2potential to convert these diagnoses into staged potentials for action-chunk value learning, providing dense supervision for policy updates while preserving the task's optimal policy under standard shaping assumptions. Measured learning progress guides further generation and program refinement. Across four manipulation task families, CoDA improves out-of-distribution success from 5.3% after supervised fine-tuning to 27.6%, exceeding the strongest closed-loop baseline by 9.5 percentage points with the same generation budget. On LIBERO-Pro, 15 rounds of generation and training raise success from 25% to 84% under position perturbations and from 1% to 76% under instruction perturbations.

### 9. [Grounding Generative Verification in 3D Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark, reasoning, multimodal, large language models, analyze verification across fine-grained, verification across fine-grained factual
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360v1>

**原始摘要**

Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and 3D generation have expanded 3D understanding and synthesis. However, beyond visual fidelity, task-specific correctness grounded in 3D evidence remains a major challenge. We argue that building reliable 3D systems requires not only stronger generation and reasoning, but also verification feedback that identifies unmet requirements and explains their violations, allowing systems to reflect on and refine their outputs. Such feedback must integrate multi-view observations and distinguish genuine inconsistencies from projection-dependent appearances. We make three contributions. First, we introduce 3DVerifierBench, a benchmark spanning 10 tasks that evaluates decisions, rejection explanations, and responses to controlled changes in specifications and candidates. A human study reveals a 21.6 percentage-point gap between humans and the strongest tested model, GPT-6 Astra, while explanation-aware analysis exposes weaknesses in rejection reliability. Second, we analyze verification across fine-grained factual categories, exposing errors in values and target binding. We develop a scalable pipeline combining multi-view fact extraction, controlled 3D editing, and native annotations to produce 14,126 training examples with contradiction explanations and linked counterfactuals. Third, we examine how verification could support 3D generation through candidate selection and recursive refinement. In human-labeled sixteen-candidate pools, oracle selection improves complete factual success from 26.7% to 90.0%. In a separate eight-input study, human-guided recursive refinement satisfies all seven requirements for every input, compared with three under parallel Oracle@8. Together, our benchmark, analysis, and data pipeline establish a foundation for models to reflect on 3D content through reliable verification feedback.

### 10. [PathFM: Fast Generative Forecasting of Multivariate Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22358v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：pathfm achieves, attention, foundation models, established forecasting benchmarks fev-bench, forecasting model that USES, makes generative TIME series
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22358v1>

**原始摘要**

Time series foundation models are becoming increasingly popular for general-purpose forecasting. However, most leading models predict marginal quantiles, leaving the dependence structure of future trajectories unspecified. This distinction matters for trajectory-dependent quantities such as cumulative demand, peak load, and event timing, yet point and marginal scores do not reveal it. To model this dependence, we introduce PathFM, a trajectory-based forecasting model that uses flow matching to generate complete future paths. We further distill the model to one or two steps and share history attention cache across draws. Together, these enable PathFM to generate multiple paths for a single series in roughly the time needed for one query to a quantile model. The distilled sampler also makes it practical to optimize a calibration loss directly. PathFM achieves competitive marginal accuracy on the established forecasting benchmarks fev-bench and TIME. To complement these evaluations, we introduce Forecast Path Score Bench (fps-bench), which augments fev-bench tasks with energy and variogram scores and downstream metrics. PathFM achieves a higher aggregate path score on fps-bench than all evaluated external baselines. Experiments on chaotic systems further show that our flow model sustains long-run variability, while strong baselines rapidly collapse. PathFM makes generative time series forecasting both competitive and practical, enabling diverse downstream applications.

### 11. [DK-VLM: Geometry as Position for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22356v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：attention, spatial reasoning, vision-language, further improves multi-step spatial, dk-vlm with dual-kernel, reasoning while the FULL
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22356v1>

**原始摘要**

Vision-language models still struggle to understand 3D spatial relations across views and update spatial judgments as the observer moves. A common approach fuses 3D reconstruction features into visual tokens, but aligning these features with pretrained semantic representations remains challenging under limited spatial supervision. We propose DK-VLM with Dual-Kernel Attention, which computes attention from both a visual token's input position and its geometric position in the scene. A semantic kernel retains the pretrained model's native attention and positional encoding, while a separate geometric kernel uses estimated 3D points and camera poses to directly modulate attention through point displacements and relative view transformations. Then, a learned gate fuses their outputs. Geometry thus enters attention as position rather than as fused reconstruction features. We further introduce Reference-Frame Composition (RC) supervision to train the model to update its reference frame across successive judgments. It provides stepwise turn and heading targets derived from verified single-step annotations. Among the compared methods, DK-VLM-8B achieves the highest average score across six spatial benchmarks and the highest overall scores on VSI-Bench (74.33) and ReVSI-32 (58.28). RC supervision further improves multi-step spatial reasoning, while the full model preserves general video understanding. Project page: https://sumuru789.github.io/DK-VLM-website/.

### 12. [Rethinking Routing Exploration in Modern Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22353v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：MoE, exploration dilemma, fine-grained expert segmentation, exploration space through token-adaptive, modern mixture-of-experts. mixture-of-experts, experts can MAKE routing
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22353v1>

**原始摘要**

Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models have become an important approach for scaling model parameters. Modern MoE architectures increasingly combine two design choices: fine-grained expert segmentation and shared experts. The former increases expressiveness by enabling more expert combinations at a fixed activation ratio, and the latter introduces experts that are active for every token. We identify an exploration dilemma between these designs: shared experts can make routing less exploratory by reducing the variability of token representations. This conflicts with the greater need for exploration induced by fine-grained expert segmentation due to the combinatorially enlarged routing space. Consequently, the additional expressiveness offered by fine-grained segmentation may not be fully realized in practice. MirrorMoE is proposed to reconcile this exploration dilemma by shrinking the exploration space through token-adaptive suppression of unnecessary experts. MirrorMoE consistently improves performance across model scales.

### 13. [SUPRA-TEX: 3D Generation with Super-Aligned Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22352v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：alignment, transformer, datasets supra-tex achieves SOTA, public datasets supra-tex achieves, super-aligned texture. single image, end-to-end asset generation matching
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22352v1>

**原始摘要**

Single image 3D generation aims to dress a given 3D mesh with relightable materials while faithfully preserving the visual details of a reference image.
