For a fixed 289-token request with three fields on one RTX 4090, mean latency is 44.16 ms for the 4B model and approximately 33--34 ms for the smaller models. On a separate 96-case known-distribution pilot, temperature scaling reduces expected multiclass Brier from 0.628 to 0.550 and expected calibration error from 0.213 to 0.089. A confidence-based handoff study on 111 public Jevbench-Hard items improves accuracy from 82/111 (73.87%) to 97/111 (87.39%) by routing 42 items to Atria-Dawn-Preview. We also describe qualitative game, visual interface, and browser demonstrations. These results support compact decision models as a practical component of larger agent systems.

### 102. [DropKV: Decoupling Residual-Output Perturbation for Near-Optimal KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22130v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：eviction methods, attention, large language models, independent per-token scoring dropkv, per-token scoring dropkv circumvents, perturbation for near-optimal kv-cache
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22130v1>

**原始摘要**

Key-value (KV) cache management has become a critical bottleneck for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) to long-context scenarios due to linear memory growth. While recent eviction methods have moved beyond simple attention-score heuristics to incorporate value representations, they typically face a fundamental trade-off: the underlying optimization for minimizing output perturbation is an NP-hard combinatorial problem, leading to mathematically suboptimal heuristics or implementations that are difficult to kernelize. In this work, we propose DropKV, a simple principled eviction framework based on Decoupled Residual-Output Perturbation. By decoupling the joint eviction decision into independent per-token scoring, DropKV circumvents combinatorial intractability and admits a provable constant-factor approximation guarantee under a cone condition that we empirically verify on three long-context LLMs. To ensure practical viability, we provide a high-performance implementation using fused Triton kernels that avoid materializing the full attention matrix, delivering a 19.8x scoring-kernel speedup that translates to up to a 9.9% matched-batch end-to-end prefill speedup. Extensive evaluations on the RULER and LongBench benchmarks demonstrate that DropKV consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines at equivalent cache budgets, achieving the lowest inference latency among eviction methods while also remaining faster than the dynamic cache baseline.

### 103. [Explaining the Roles of Experts in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs for Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22127v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：MoE LLMS, dense LLMS, personalization features., large language models, shared with the non-personalized, experts moves the non-personalized
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22127v1>

**原始摘要**

Personalization of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly important for user-centric applications, yet prior methods mainly adapt dense LLMs, whose polysemantic neurons, each encoding multiple unrelated concepts, hinder localizing the components behind personalization, and thus understanding and controlling it. We propose to personalize Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs, which sparsely route each token to a few specialized experts, reducing polysemanticity and enabling such localization. We adapt three open-weight MoE LLMs on a large-scale personalization dataset and train crosscoders on paired activations of the personalized and non-personalized models to learn interpretable personalized latents, from which we identify the experts encoding distinct personalization features. The personalized MoE LLMs perform competitively with dense LLMs while activating only a fraction of their parameters, exhibit lower polysemanticity than dense LLMs with similar active parameters, and, within the same model family, yield personalized latents that are less shared with the non-personalized model. Causal interventions show that these latents suffice to recover the personalized model's behavior and that boosting their associated experts moves the non-personalized model toward it, revealing distinct routing paths for different personalization features.

### 104. [Causal Integrated Gradients: Scalable Feature Attribution Along Causal Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22126v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：causal structure, integrated gradients, causal graph, causal integrated, feature attribution, benchmark
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22126v1>

**原始摘要**

Existing feature attribution methods that account for feature dependencies do not scale to high dimensions. We propose causal integrated gradients (CIG) and causal expected gradients (CEG), which extend integrated gradients to incorporate prior causal structure among input features while retaining its computational efficiency and, under conditions on the causal graph, its axiomatic guarantees. In many settings, some causal structure between features is already known, for example, that a transcription factor causally regulates a target gene's expression. Yet existing gradient-based methods cannot exploit this structure, instead crediting a feature's downstream effects over its true upstream cause, such as ranking the target gene above the transcription factor that regulates it. Our key idea is to embed this causal structure by routing the integration path along the causal graph. The method thereby decomposes each feature's attribution into a direct effect and effects mediated through its causal descendants. We evaluate CIG in three settings of increasing realism. First, on a synthetic benchmark with a known causal model, CIG's attributions best match the ground-truth causal effect from Pearl's do-calculus, matching the accuracy of causal Shapley values at a fraction of the computational cost. Second, on simulated single-cell gene expression data with a known gene regulatory network (Dyngen), CIG most accurately recovers the transcription factors driving the simulation. Third, on real interferon-$β$ stimulation data, where only an approximate causal graph is available, CIG better prioritizes the transcription factors driving the measured response than DeepSHAP and integrated gradients. Together, these results show that incorporating causal structure into gradient-based attributions recovers upstream drivers that existing methods miss, without sacrificing scalability.

### 105. [PROBE: Learning to Audit Policy Compliance in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22124v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, policy violations, policy auditing for real-world, broader and higher-yield policy, through multi-turn, users and probe THEM
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22124v1>

**原始摘要**

Modern agentic systems extend language models with external tools, enabling them to complete complex tasks through multi-turn interactions. As these agents are deployed in consequential settings, they are often constrained by policies governing pricing, privacy, confirmation, safety, and other requirements. However, existing evaluation methods leave a critical gap: capability benchmarks measure what agents can accomplish, while content-safety red-teaming measures harmful generations, but neither tests whether agents comply with predefined policies during otherwise valid tool use. Such policy violations are difficult to audit because their effects may emerge only after several turns, span multiple tool calls, or surface in downstream systems. We propose PROBE (Policy Rule Observation via Behavioral Exploration), an algorithm for training LLM-based auditors that interact with deployed agents as realistic users and probe them for policy violations through multi-turn, tool-mediated dialogue. We formalize agent auditing as a two-player Markov game and propose a composite reward that balances violation discovery, behavioral plausibility, conversation progress, and policy diversity. To study training objectives and reward design, we compare GRPO, DPO, and GFlowNet-based optimization in a controlled setting. Across extensive experiments, auditors trained with PROBE uncover substantially broader and higher-yield policy violations than same-architecture zero-shot adversaries, even when the auditor is significantly smaller than the target agent. Our results show that multi-turn behavioral auditing is a distinct and necessary axis of agent evaluation, complementing capability and safety benchmarks. PROBE offers a scalable path toward automated policy auditing for real-world agentic systems and suggests a co-evolutionary loop in which auditors expose failures and agents improve from auditor feedback.

## 具身智能 / 强化学习

### 1. [LIBERO-PeRM: Benchmarking Personalized Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22372v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：personalized robotic, robotic manipulation, benchmark, agents. libero-perm studies FIVE, libero-perm studies FIVE questions, embodied agents. libero-perm studies
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22372v1>

**原始摘要**

Personalization is essential for general-purpose robots to transition into household environments and achieve true practicality. While general robotic manipulation has been extensively studied, personalized robotic manipulation (PeRM) lacks a controlled benchmark for separating task completion from preference satisfaction. We introduce LIBERO-PeRM, a benchmark for preference learning in embodied agents. LIBERO-PeRM studies five questions: 1) how policies follow preferences in zero-shot settings; 2) how they learn single preferences from demonstrations; 3) how efficiently they acquire preferences as demonstrations increase; 4) how learned preferences generalize across layouts and tasks; and 5) how policies compose preferences and behave under conflicts. We propose a controlled formulation and taxonomy of observable preferences in simulation, covering motion, skill, and user-profile choices. This formulation is restricted to language-expressible preferences that can be instantiated in LIBERO without changing the task goal, evaluated automatically, and reused across tasks. Building on LIBERO, our procedural pipeline generates paired demonstrations with and without preferences. We create five task suites (312 tasks) with high-quality demonstrations, rich metadata, and preference-satisfaction metrics. Experiments show that current VLA policies struggle with zero-shot personalization, while SFT makes preferences learnable. Mixed training achieves better no-preference retention than sequential adaptation and improves performance with contextual prompts compared with preference-only training, but can weaken motion-preference learning; preference generalization, decomposition, and conflict handling remain difficult. The code, datasets, and demos can be found on the project website: https://libero-perm.github.io/.

### 2. [Value as Memory: Online Value-Guided Post-training for General Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22368v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：value caching, online autonomous learning, reinforcement learning, caching and online real-world, post-training for general robot, task-progress memory while online
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22368v1>

**原始摘要**

Real-world post-training of generalist robot policies faces two challenges: partial observability makes action selection ambiguous, while offline post-training methods delay feedback from deployment experience. We present Value as Memory (VaM), a framework that addresses these challenges through value caching and online real-world reinforcement learning (RL). Value caching conditions actions on recent value predictions as task-progress memory, while online updates enable timely policy improvement by combining offline demonstrations, off-policy interventions, and on-policy rollouts for sample-efficient adaptation. As a result, VaM transitions from offline RL warmup to online autonomous learning with sparse rewards. Across long-horizon tasks involving deformable objects and contact-rich manipulation, VaM yields a mean absolute success-rate gain of $29.4\%$ with value caching, while achieving a $32.8\%$ gain with online autonomous learning over matched offline training. The evaluated online schedule performs $8\times$ as many policy iterations under the same gradient-step budget, with more than $2\times$ lower recorded time per gradient step with concurrent data collection and training.

### 3. [Discovering state-of-the-art quantum circuits with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22367v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：large language models, large-scale quantum information processors, discovering state-of-the-art quantum circuits, BUT realizing useful quantum, circuits with large language, quantum simulation and number
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22367v1>

**原始摘要**

Significant efforts are currently being directed toward the implementation of large-scale quantum information processors, but realizing useful quantum advantage also requires the development of efficient quantum algorithms. While for many practical problems the dominant resource costs are associated with a small set of computational primitives, optimizing them in the quantum setting presents unique challenges, including a reversibility constraint absent from ordinary classical computation. Here we introduce an evolutionary framework in which large language models (LLMs) propose circuit designs and an independent evaluator verifies and scores them. We apply this framework to tasks spanning numerical computation, chemistry, optimization, quantum simulation, and number theory. In seven of the eight tasks, the discovered circuits significantly reduce both Toffoli and qubit counts relative to the state of the art. In the eighth, the search discovers a new reversible architecture for prime-field modular inversion. By handling reversibility differently, the architecture makes divsteps -- a classical algorithm previously impractical for reversible integer arithmetic -- viable and substantially improves the Toffoli-qubit trade-off for modular inversion. These results open the door for evaluator-guided LLM co-design of new algorithmic structures for quantum computation.

### 4. [T2-z: Test-Time Optimization of Environment Latents for Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22364v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：t2-z, environment latents, test-time optimization, robotic world, optimization stage, world models
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22364v1>

**原始摘要**

Predicting how the world responds to an action requires more than what can be seen: robots must account for environmental factors that are neither directly observable nor explicitly specified, from physical properties such as friction and mass to human-defined environmental rules. We introduce T2-Z for test-time optimization of environment latents, which augments a robotic world model with a learnable environment latent to condition world predictions. This compact latent captures underlying environmental variation without supervision of what environmental factors exist or what values they take. During training, T2-Z alternates between two stages: a world-model optimization stage that fixes the latents and learns general action-conditioned dynamics across environments; and an environment-latent optimization stage that freezes the world model and optimizes the conditioning latents, allowing latent structure to emerge purely through world prediction. At test time, the world model remains frozen and a newly initialized environment latent is optimized from one or more test-time interactions, adapting the model to previously unseen environmental configurations. Across 7 simulated settings and 4 real-world robotic tasks, T2-Z reduces object prediction error metric from 15.20 to 7.00 and raises action success from 33.73% to 72.96%. Its emergent latents capture structured representations of individual and compounded factors across diverse visual scenes. Ablations further show that T2-Z can efficiently infer environment latents from as little as a single test-time interaction trajectory.

### 5. [Perceive, Predict, and Plan: a Unified World Action Model for Egocentric Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22363v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：action model for egocentric, unified world action, egocentric dexterous, transformer, attention, diffusion
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22363v1>

**原始摘要**

We introduce P$^{3}$-WAM, a unified world action model for egocentric dexterous manipulation that learns 3D perception, action-conditioned prediction, and goal-directed planning through three complementary modes: Perceive, which reconstructs articulated hand motion and camera trajectories from video; Predict, which generates visual futures under prescribed actions; and Plan, which jointly generates action trajectories and their anticipated visual consequences conditioned on an initial image, a language instruction, and an optional visual goal. Our model integrates a compact, structured 3D action representation into a pretrained video diffusion transformer through a mixture-of-transformers architecture with shared base weights, modality-specific adaptation, and bidirectional visual-action attention. Randomized modality visibility lets video and 3D actions serve as either conditioning context or prediction targets across the three modes. Experiments show that unified training improves all three capabilities over single-mode training, including gains in planning quality under a vision-language model evaluation. Through unified training, our model outperforms specialized baselines on 3D hand reconstruction and action-conditioned video prediction across four datasets, while producing goal-directed manipulation videos with stronger instruction adherence and action progression than video-generation baselines. Project Page: https://p3-wam.pages.dev/

### 6. [CoDA: Co-Evolution of Data Engines and Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22362v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-tuning, vision-language, action policy co-evolve through, CODA improves out-of-distribution success, families CODA improves out-of-distribution, engines and action policies.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22362v1>

**原始摘要**

Generative simulation can automate robot environment construction, but physically valid environments are not necessarily useful for training. The challenge is to identify environments that address a policy's failures and supervision that helps it overcome them. We introduce CoDA, a framework in which a simulation data engine and an action policy co-evolve through a shared heterogeneous memory of environment construction, policy failures, and learning progress. The memory compiles validated diagnoses from a vision-language critic into generation programs that recreate difficulties and training programs that help the policy overcome them. We further propose Text2potential to convert these diagnoses into staged potentials for action-chunk value learning, providing dense supervision for policy updates while preserving the task's optimal policy under standard shaping assumptions. Measured learning progress guides further generation and program refinement. Across four manipulation task families, CoDA improves out-of-distribution success from 5.3% after supervised fine-tuning to 27.6%, exceeding the strongest closed-loop baseline by 9.5 percentage points with the same generation budget. On LIBERO-Pro, 15 rounds of generation and training raise success from 25% to 84% under position perturbations and from 1% to 76% under instruction perturbations.

### 7. [Grounding Generative Verification in 3D Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark, reasoning, multimodal, large language models, analyze verification across fine-grained, verification across fine-grained factual
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360v1>

**原始摘要**

Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and 3D generation have expanded 3D understanding and synthesis. However, beyond visual fidelity, task-specific correctness grounded in 3D evidence remains a major challenge. We argue that building reliable 3D systems requires not only stronger generation and reasoning, but also verification feedback that identifies unmet requirements and explains their violations, allowing systems to reflect on and refine their outputs. Such feedback must integrate multi-view observations and distinguish genuine inconsistencies from projection-dependent appearances. We make three contributions. First, we introduce 3DVerifierBench, a benchmark spanning 10 tasks that evaluates decisions, rejection explanations, and responses to controlled changes in specifications and candidates. A human study reveals a 21.6 percentage-point gap between humans and the strongest tested model, GPT-6 Astra, while explanation-aware analysis exposes weaknesses in rejection reliability. Second, we analyze verification across fine-grained factual categories, exposing errors in values and target binding. We develop a scalable pipeline combining multi-view fact extraction, controlled 3D editing, and native annotations to produce 14,126 training examples with contradiction explanations and linked counterfactuals. Third, we examine how verification could support 3D generation through candidate selection and recursive refinement. In human-labeled sixteen-candidate pools, oracle selection improves complete factual success from 26.7% to 90.0%. In a separate eight-input study, human-guided recursive refinement satisfies all seven requirements for every input, compared with three under parallel Oracle@8. Together, our benchmark, analysis, and data pipeline establish a foundation for models to reflect on 3D content through reliable verification feedback.

### 8. [PathFM: Fast Generative Forecasting of Multivariate Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22358v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：pathfm achieves, attention, foundation models, established forecasting benchmarks fev-bench, forecasting model that USES, makes generative TIME series
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22358v1>

**原始摘要**

Time series foundation models are becoming increasingly popular for general-purpose forecasting. However, most leading models predict marginal quantiles, leaving the dependence structure of future trajectories unspecified. This distinction matters for trajectory-dependent quantities such as cumulative demand, peak load, and event timing, yet point and marginal scores do not reveal it. To model this dependence, we introduce PathFM, a trajectory-based forecasting model that uses flow matching to generate complete future paths. We further distill the model to one or two steps and share history attention cache across draws. Together, these enable PathFM to generate multiple paths for a single series in roughly the time needed for one query to a quantile model. The distilled sampler also makes it practical to optimize a calibration loss directly. PathFM achieves competitive marginal accuracy on the established forecasting benchmarks fev-bench and TIME. To complement these evaluations, we introduce Forecast Path Score Bench (fps-bench), which augments fev-bench tasks with energy and variogram scores and downstream metrics. PathFM achieves a higher aggregate path score on fps-bench than all evaluated external baselines. Experiments on chaotic systems further show that our flow model sustains long-run variability, while strong baselines rapidly collapse. PathFM makes generative time series forecasting both competitive and practical, enabling diverse downstream applications.

### 9. [OmniVerifier-C1: Decomposed Graded Verifier for Rule-Governed Generative World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22355v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：rule-governed generative world, reinforcement learning, graded verifier, multimodal, alignment, reasoning
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22355v1>

**原始摘要**

Generative visual verifiers play a crucial role in advancing multimodal intelligence. To enhance the robustness of visual verifiers in complex multimodal scenarios involving compositional requirements, we move beyond conventional binary verifiers that produce only True/False discrete judgments and introduce a verifier that automatically decomposes complex prompts or environmental rules into fine-grained components and assigns pointwise scores to provide a continuous and more informative critique signal. Our experiments further demonstrate that these structured and interpretable outputs improve the verifier's generalization to out-of-domain multimodal critic tasks. To train this verifier, we introduce a Decoupled RL training strategy that jointly optimizes sub-question-level binary judgment and global alignment scoring, using precise local feedback to develop fine-grained verification capabilities that provide a foundation for reliable continuous scoring at the global level. Under a general reinforcement learning framework, we first empirically demonstrate that the proposed pointwise graded verifier significantly outperforms binary verifiers in traditional text-to-image scenarios. We then extend our investigation to rule-governed generative world modeling where generative models are leveraged for world simulation and world reasoning under explicit rules. From a theoretical standpoint, we further show that a pairwise verification paradigm yields more accurate critiques than the pointwise formulation when ground-truth images are available, with empirical results on diverse structured world-modeling tasks such as Maze and Sudoku further validating the effectiveness of our approach. Overall, our findings suggest a key insight: the transition from generative models to structured world-modeling agents critically hinges on the availability of accurate pairwise graded visual verifiers.

### 10. [FDE-Gym: The Last Mile of AI Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22350v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, benchmark, fde-gym the LAST MILE, experts from real-world delivery, working solutions. fde-gym scores, deliver working solutions. fde-gym
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22350v1>

**原始摘要**

Existing agent benchmarks typically evaluate agents on well-specified tasks defined by issue reports, specifications, or task tickets. Real-world customer delivery is different: forward-deployed engineers (FDEs) must clarify ambiguous requirements, work in live systems, and uncover unstated constraints. We introduce FDE-Gym, a benchmark of 95 end-to-end delivery tasks from 20 customer scenarios across 18 sectors, each adapted by industry experts from real-world delivery projects. In simulated customer environments with scripted stakeholders and modifiable customer systems, agents interact with stakeholders and operate on production-like systems to deliver working solutions. FDE-Gym scores a delivery by how much it improves the customer's own success metric, normalized between 0 (unchanged system) and 1 (reference solution), with penalties for stakeholder interactions (messages, trial runs, and waiting time). An online platform lets humans practice FDE skills under the same grading criteria. We evaluate 13 model configurations from seven providers. In the realistic setting, where unstated constraints must be discovered, the best configuration achieves a mean FDE-Gym score of 0.56 and resolves only 14 of the 95 tasks (a score of at least 0.8 with no incident). Discovering unstated constraints is the primary bottleneck: performance drops sharply when models must uncover them without explicit guidance. Yet information discovery alone does not account for the gap: even on the 23 tasks where the best configuration obtains every key fact from stakeholders, it reaches a normalized business uplift of 0.8 or above on only 10. These results reveal a substantial gap between solving well-specified tasks and delivering effective AI systems for real customers.

### 11. [MLExpert: Towards a Generalist Agentic Model for Autonomous Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22344v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, machine learning, mlexpert improves, large language models, method for autonomous long-horizon, post-training method for autonomous
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22344v1>

**原始摘要**

Large language models (LLMs) are expanding from assisting scientific research and engineering to autonomously developing and improving AI systems, advancing AI for AI (AI4AI) as a pathway toward recursive self-improvement (RSI). Workflow-driven machine learning engineering (MLE) systems provide a practical path toward this pursuit through complex orchestration that manages long-horizon experimentation, while recent MLE systems adopt model-directed interaction, allowing models to autonomously organize experiments and adapt optimization strategies based on execution feedback. However, feedback from each experiment reflects several preceding decisions, and a long MLE run contains many such costly experiments. Training must therefore select useful supervision from long interaction trajectories and learn from intermediate outcomes. We propose a post-training method for autonomous long-horizon MLE optimization and apply it to train MLExpert, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts agentic model. We design the Kaggle-MLE Factory to construct executable tasks and an interaction harness for model-directed experimentation. Hierarchical agentic SFT transfers general tool-use and coding capabilities to MLE through outcome-guided supervision. Windowed Interaction RL (WIRL) treats the multi-turn decisions between successive experiment results as an execution window and assigns it a shared outcome-based reward. Asynchronous collection and policy correction support policy updates before the trajectory ends. On MLE-Bench Lite, MLExpert improves the average medal rate of the base model from 37.9% to 72.7%, achieving performance comparable to larger frontier models such as Kimi-K3 and GLM-5.3. Further experiments show that MLExpert improves performance on NatureBench Lite, agent scaffold optimization, and algorithm optimization, suggesting that the learned optimization capability generalizes to other tasks.

### 12. [GeoState: Learning View-Decoupled Geometry States for 3D World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22343v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：view-decoupled geometry, world models, alignment, autoregressive, foundation models, modeling that separates scene-state
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22343v1>

**原始摘要**

Recent 3D world models increasingly use features from geometry foundation models (GFMs) as predictive world states, benefiting from their rich geometric priors. However, the view dependence of these features entangles scene dynamics with camera-induced variation, making the resulting state evolution harder to predict. We propose GeoState, a view-decoupled geometry representation for predictive 3D world modeling that separates scene-state evolution from camera-dependent observation. Specifically, a scene tokenizer aggregates multi-view GFM features into a view-decoupled scene state, which a geometry decoder queries using camera rays. An autoregressive world model then predicts future scene states, while camera motion is modeled separately. The predicted states can therefore be decoded under either predicted or externally specified cameras, while retaining a single context-derived scale throughout the rollout without per-frame scale alignment. Experiments show that GeoState outperforms prior 3D world models on Waymo, supports geometrically consistent cross-view querying, and generalizes zero-shot to KITTI and nuScenes. Project page: https://hongchang-liu.github.io/GeoState-project-page/

### 13. [Spark-0: Scaling Spatially Aligned Tactile Pretraining for Robot Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22342v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：spark-0, spatially aligned, benchmark, large-scale visual pretraining BUT, full-hand tactile human manipulation, pretraining image-space contact MAPS
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22342v1>

**原始摘要**

Generalist robot policies benefit from large-scale visual pretraining but often struggle with contact-rich dexterous manipulation, where force, compliance, and incipient slip are hard to observe from images. We present Spark-0, a spatially aligned vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) model that uses touch in two complementary ways. During pretraining, image-space contact maps derived from tactile gloves, together with features from a frozen 3D geometry teacher, supervise the visual tokens; these objectives are removed at inference. In parallel, a tactile pathway encodes regional force histories and 3D taxel states to condition the action expert. A single checkpoint therefore supports both vision-only and tactile-conditioned deployment. To scale this supervision, we build HumanTouch, a 1,000-hour dataset of full-hand tactile human manipulation, and pretrain Spark-0 on it together with egocentric video, robot data, and simulation. We also release SparkArena, which pairs vision-only simulated tasks with tactile-conditioned real-robot tasks on one dexterous embodiment. Spark-0 achieves the highest mean success rate on both SparkArena tracks and ranks first on all eight aggregate metrics of the Ego Humanoid Manipulation Benchmark. Compared with vision-only pretraining on the same data and compute with touch discarded, retaining the touch of 1,000 hours of HumanTouch raises vision-only success on Ego Humanoid from 52.5% to 71.7%, and success on SparkArena-Real, where touch is also used at deployment, from 44.0% to 52.0%, indicating that touch serves not only as a policy input but also as scalable supervision for dexterous visuomotor pretraining. The Website is available at: https://xsparkai.com/Spark-0.

### 14. [3DCodeVerse: Exploring 3D Worlds as Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22341v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：human feedback, dcodeverse harness, agent, reasoning, fine-tuning, scale. dcodeverse comprises large-scale
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22341v1>

**原始摘要**

We introduce 3DCodeVerse, a research ecosystem that enables coding agents to create editable and verifiable 3D content at scale. 3DCodeVerse comprises large-scale, high-quality datasets of executable 3D code, a coding harness for spatial reasoning and automated verification, and practical guidance for 3D coding. First, we construct 3DCodeVerse-2M, a large-scale curated dataset of standalone 3D executable code. We build this corpus by collecting open-source data, converting specialized representations, and generating new programs with coding agents. Each program is packaged with the source files and associated assets required for execution in its declared environment, along with available descriptions and verification records. Second, we develop the 3DCodeVerse Harness, a multi-agent directed acyclic graph framework for generating and refining 3D content. It uses execution checks, spatial measurements, and visual feedback to automatically evaluate and revise generated programs without human feedback during generation. Third, we conduct systematic empirical studies of 3D coding, establishing baselines with fine-tuned open models and deriving practical guidance through component-level analyses of our harness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that fine-tuning on our dataset significantly improves the 3D coding capabilities of open-source models. Notably, our 3DCodeVerse harness achieves a 90% win rate against Claude Design (3D Object) in pairwise human evaluations, when both are utilized by Claude Opus 5.5.

### 15. [Dexterity from Supervised Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22340v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：dexterity from supervised, reinforcement learning, PLAY policy, supervised PLAY, world model., inference the SAME PLAY
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22340v1>

**原始摘要**

How can robots autonomously collect the contact-rich experience needed to learn dexterous manipulation? We present DSP: Dexterity from supervised play, a framework in which a robot learns a play policy from minutes of human play through behavior cloning, then deploys it autonomously to collect hours of interaction data to train a world model. During inference, the same play policy then guides model-predictive control (MPC) by proposing candidate action sequences for evaluation under the learned world model. To accelerate inference, we also introduce a method for distilling a closed-loop, goal-conditioned policy through self-play within the learned world model. We evaluate this framework on underactuated pushing and any-to-any in-hand pose reorientation with a multifingered hand, in simulation and real world. In real world experiment, our method outperforms hindsight behavior cloning by 69.6% in success rate and achieves comparable success to reinforcement learning with 490 times less interaction data. These results demonstrate that supervised play can autonomously collect interaction data, guide model-based planning, and enable policy learning without any task-specific demonstrations or sim-to-real transfer.

### 16. [Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Path-Safe Target Reaching in Robotic Harvesting of Hydroponic Lettuce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22337v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：robotic harvesting, reinforcement learning, target reaching, benchmark, fine-tuning, ur10e robotic ARM without
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22337v1>

**原始摘要**

Robotic harvesting requires precise manipulation to ensure accurate positioning of the end-effector, especially in a constraint workspace. This study introduces the Path-Safe Approach Proximal Policy Optimization (PSA-PPO), a target-conditioned local reaching framework designed for robotic harvesting of nutrient film technique (NFT)-hydroponic lettuce in greenhouses. The proposed PSA-PPO preserves the standard clipped PPO objective while introducing a harvesting-oriented policy formulation that integrates target-conditioned observations, corridor-aware spatial constraints, trajectory-history-based safety encoding, and task-specific success criteria for constrained robotic manipulation. PSAPPO was trained entirely in Isaac Lab and successfully transferred to a physical UR10e robotic arm without any real-world policy fine-tuning. In a controlled physical benchmark against 150 scheduled targets, PSA-PPO achieved the highest reaching rate of 94.7%, outperforming Cartesian+TOTG (86.0%), RRTConnect (88.0%), and RRT* (80.0%), while reducing median RMS jerk by 39.8 to 73.0% relative to the classical planners during the final approach segment. A formulation ablation evaluated over 200 simulation targets demonstrates that path-safety and side-corridor shaping are the principal drivers of harvesting-compatible performance: removing either term substantially degrades strict success even when terminal endpoint accuracy is preserved or improved, confirming that optimization toward the endpoint alone is an insufficient objective for constrained crop-proximal manipulation. Finally, PSA-PPO was integrated with an RGB-D vision module and an end-effector and evaluated as a complete greenhouse harvesting system across 144 lettuce harvesting attempts, achieving 94.4% vision-guided target reaching and 86.1% complete harvesting success.

### 17. [Robust Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Treatment Regimes under Model and Measurement Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22336v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, adaptive treatment, robust reinforcement, accurate and the disease, under model and measurement, findings suggest that robust
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22336v1>

**原始摘要**

We develop a robust reinforcement learning (RL) framework for learning adaptive treatment policies that generalize across patients and remain reliable under errors in the measured biomarker and shifts in the disease dynamics. By modeling the patient as a hidden context, we address generalization and robustness in a unified manner through robust contextual Markov decision processes. Applying our framework to adaptive therapy for metastatic prostate cancer in a simulator calibrated to clinical data, we obtain generalizable and robust policies that substantially outperform the existing adaptive therapy method. In particular, we attain robustness with almost no loss of performance when the biomarker is accurate and the disease evolves as anticipated, and find that the resulting policy Pareto-dominates the existing method across a diverse array of scenarios. Our findings suggest that robust RL holds great promise as a principled methodology for learning reliable adaptive treatment policies.

### 18. [Illumination-Aware Active Perception for Spacecraft Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22335v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：spacecraft inspection., line-of-sight inspection metrics, illumination-aware active perception, BAND and for purely, compared with for geometric, spacecraft materials and TEND
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22335v1>

**原始摘要**

Harsh and rapidly varying orbital illumination introduces failure modes during spacecraft inspection. Existing planners rely on line-of-sight inspection metrics which perform occlusion checks against a known target and conflate geometric visibility with task-relevant information. These metrics overlook illumination effects including eclipse, self-shadowing, and specular reflection from spacecraft materials and tend to declare components as inspected despite the corresponding images lacking sufficient information. We propose an inspection-planning framework that replaces geometric visibility with a learned measure of camera information derived from a classifier variational autoencoder (VAE) that operates directly on the captured image. The encoder model is trained to classify spacecraft surface components from rendered patches, and its total latent variance is used to learn the varying illumination of said components, replacing binary visibility checks with a continuous, image-grounded quantity. We evaluate the approach by implementing a Guidance, Navigation, and Control architecture in a photorealistic physics simulator, and vary lighting and viewing geometries with multiple orbits. We show that, although all approaches successfully maximize their own objective, our approach results in better illumination: a mean brightness of $0.57$, with $81.6\%$ of observations above $0.25$, compared with $0.02$ ($3.0\%$) for geometric planning with an illumination band and $0.04$ ($6.0\%$) for purely geometric planning.

### 19. [KnowGPO: Knowledge-Grouped Policy Optimization for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22331v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, DEEP search, grouped for within-group comparison, search agents solve knowledge-intensive, knowgpo consistently outperforms outcome-only, knowgpo knowledge-grouped policy optimization
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22331v1>

**原始摘要**

Deep search agents solve knowledge-intensive tasks through long-horizon, multi-turn interactions, where assigning credit to individual search decisions is fundamentally difficult. Outcome-based reinforcement learning provides only a single terminal reward shared across an entire trajectory. Recent process-supervision methods aim to assign credit at finer granularity, but they typically anchor credit to either surface-level trajectory states or model-internal signals, both of which become unreliable in deep search where observations are heterogeneous and retrieved contexts are long and noisy. We propose KnowGPO, a reinforcement learning framework that aligns trajectories by the task-relevant knowledge they have acquired rather than by their surface observations. For each query, KnowGPO decomposes the information required to derive the answer into atomic knowledge conditions, and represents the state of a trajectory at each step by the subset of conditions it has satisfied. Trajectories sharing the same knowledge state are grouped for within-group comparison, and the incremental knowledge gain between consecutive steps serves as an environment-grounded process reward. Experiments on Xbench, BrowseComp-ZH, and GAIA show KnowGPO consistently outperforms outcome-only (GRPO) and process-supervision baselines (GiGPO, IGPO) by 3.8 to 4.2 points, with our 8B model rivaling or exceeding most 32B open-source systems.

### 20. [Training Reasoning Models to Generate Diverse Scientific Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22324v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, agent, benchmark, reasoning, large language models, claude OPUS
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22324v1>

**原始摘要**

One of the first steps of a research pipeline is generating ideas. Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate this step, but most of this work optimizes the quality of individual ideas, while overlooking diversity. In our work, we study document-grounded ideation, focusing on increasing the diversity of LLM generated ideas and ensuring they are high quality. Given prior papers, a model must propose a set of ideas that are each high-quality and clearly distinct from one another, while also being grounded in the input documents. To optimize for this, we propose training with a set-level reinforcement learning objective. In our setup, in a single generation, the model writes K structured idea cards grounded in the source papers, and the set is scored jointly, where each idea's quality is weighted by its distance from the ideas written before it. This teaches the model to steer each new idea away from its predecessors. We show that our trained 8B model achieves higher creative utility (a measure combining quality and diversity) than Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-6 Astra on our contamination-free benchmark. In a pilot human study with four experts, our sets are judged as promising as Claude Opus 4.8's and contain marginally more unique ideas. When a coding agent runs proof-of-concept experiments on the ideas, our model's success@k is comparable to Claude Opus 4.8's, so we see that the added diversity does not cost any executability.

### 21. [Mem4Act: Learning Effective Latent Memory for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22323v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：mem4act, long-horizon robotic, learning effective latent memory, latent memory for long-horizon, memory captures BOTH high-level, memory supervision and memory-first
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22323v1>

**原始摘要**

Long-horizon robotic manipulation fundamentally requires memory: agents must retain task-relevant information from past observations and interactions and use it to guide future actions. Existing approaches typically preserve raw historical observations, compressed visual tokens, or language summaries, but these representations either expose the action policy to increasingly long histories or risk discarding task-relevant temporal, semantic, and perceptual information. We introduce Mem4Act, a framework for learning effective latent memory as a fixed-size representation explicitly optimized for long-horizon robotic control. Mem4Act addresses three coupled challenges: how to efficiently cover long histories, what information memory should retain, and how to ensure the policy actually relies on memory. To this end, it organizes history via multi-granularity temporal aggregation, preserves complementary semantic and perceptual information through question-answering supervision and key-frame DINO feature reconstruction, and employs memory-first two-stage training to encourage greater use of memory by the action policy. The resulting memory captures both high-level task state and fine-grained perceptual evidence while providing a bounded interface to the action policy. Across RoboMME, RMBench, and four real-world manipulation tasks, Mem4Act achieves state-of-the-art performance on memory-dependent control, with success rates of 49.88%, 61.0%, and 81.5%, respectively. Extensive ablations further demonstrate the importance of temporal aggregation, complementary memory supervision, and memory-first training.

### 22. [Learning Self-Orchestration in Decentralized Agent Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22322v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：decentralized agent swarms., learned self-orchestration, multi-agent, BOTH action and bidding, action policies and self-orchestration, self-orchestration supports reuse scaling
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22322v1>

**原始摘要**

The capabilities of an agent swarm depend on both its individual members and how they coordinate. Yet existing multi-agent systems often train agents within fixed orchestration structures, separating the learning of individual capabilities from the organization of collective behavior. We introduce market-based post-training (MPT), a framework for jointly learning action policies and self-orchestration in decentralized agent swarms. Agents express their willingness to participate through bids, which determine who acts and how collective outcomes provide local training credit. Learning both action and bidding behavior allows individual capabilities and patterns of participation to adapt together without a central coordinating agent. We evaluate MPT on embodied crafting, robot navigation, and deep research. Results show gains over monolithic post-training and confirm that agent participation indeed evolves with learning. With the same post-trained action policies, learned self-orchestration outperforms alternative coordination strategies. Ablations show that both action and bidding benefit from learning, and that learned bids provide more effective action-training signals. Beyond the original training objectives, trained swarms adapt faster to related tasks and achieve better performance with larger populations at inference time. Larger populations also suffer smaller performance losses under agent dropout, demonstrating that learned self-orchestration supports reuse, scaling, and robustness in decentralized agent swarms. Code and trained models are available at https://github.com/Embodied-Minds-Lab/MPT.

### 23. [AVC-Reward: Event-Centric Audio-Video Consistency Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22321v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：audio-video consistency, audio-video generation, alignment, benchmark, general formulation for audio-video, trainable audio-video reward model.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22321v1>

**原始摘要**

As joint audio-video generation models become more capable, they can generate audio-visual scenes exhibiting three forms of complexity: multi-event composition, multi-layer audio mixture, and asymmetric cross-modal relations. However, current evaluations largely reuse metrics from earlier simple and unidirectional tasks. They typically evaluate an audio-video clip as a whole and rely on simple predefined relations, making them unsuitable for these increasingly complex scenes. We propose an event-centric evaluation framework that disentangles audio and visual events at the event level, analyzes each event independently, cross-validates relations across modalities, and aggregates the judgments into a coarse-to-fine, three-level consistency metric. The metric assesses global narrative harmony, event-level temporal synchronization, and fine-grained attribute alignment, providing a general formulation for audio-video consistency evaluation across diverse generation settings and cross-modal relations. We instantiate the metric as AVC-Reward, a trainable audio-video reward model. To evaluate the agreement between automatic judgments and human perception across generation paradigms and relation complexities, we construct AVC-Benchmark, which is primarily built from diverse positive and negative samples generated by various types of audio-video generation models. AVC-Reward achieves state-of-the-art alignment with human judgment among automatic methods, provides interpretable rationales that localize failures, and improves the audio-video consistency of a joint generation model when used as an online reinforcement-learning signal. Project page: https://tsuki0512.github.io/AVC-Reward.github.io/

### 24. [A team of Gemini 4 Argon agents beats Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22319v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, gemini argon, RUN unfolded the TEAM, EASY difficulty the TEAM, difficulty the TEAM wrote, sandboxed minecraft survival world
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22319v1>

**原始摘要**

We placed four Gemini 4 Argon agents in a sandboxed Minecraft survival world and gave them one goal: defeat the Ender Dragon. Each agent controlled a player via an open-source library called Mineflayer and coordinated with its teammates through in-game chat and shared memory. No one assigned roles or wrote game logic. The agents planned, wrote, and debugged every program themselves. Researchers could watch the run on a live dashboard and pause or stop it at any time, but they had no cause to intervene. Starting from a randomly generated world on Easy difficulty, the team wrote 2,783 programs and defeated the dragon in 27 hours and 14 minutes, dying 605 times along the way. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end autonomous completion of Minecraft by AI agents, achieved using only the standard Mineflayer library without pre-scouted seeds or other game-specific tools written by humans. Winning required the agents to keep a coherent plan across thousands of dependent steps for more than a day. Complex real-world software engineering and enterprise tasks demand the same long-horizon coding and multi-agent coordination. We describe the setup, how the run unfolded, the team's strategies (dividing the work, sharing what they learned, recovering each other's items), and the limits of the result.

### 25. [Learning to Steer LLM Agents and Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22316v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, reinforcement learning, FAST PATH and HOW, GOAL drift and generate, drift and generate effective, execution resources and leads
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22316v1>

**原始摘要**

Large language model (LLM)-based agents and multi-agent systems increasingly tackle complex tasks through long-horizon tool use and inter-agent collaboration. However, these systems are prone to deviating from the overall goal as execution unfolds, which wastes execution resources and leads to undesired outcomes, a failure termed goal drift. In this work, we propose a steering agent that supervises execution on the fly and steers the system back on track when drift occurs. The steering agent observes the execution context, deciding when to intervene with a low-cost fast path and how to intervene with a high-cost slow path. To unlock its steering intelligence, the ability to assess goal drift and generate effective interventions, we jointly train the two paths with reinforcement learning, combining a pairwise ranking objective with group-relative policy optimization (GRPO). Experiments on information-seeking search tasks show that the steering agent provides effective supervision for both single-agent and multi-agent systems, improving their performance and efficiency. The learned steering intelligence generalizes to out-of-domain tasks, transfers to unseen executors, and supports controllable intervention frequency without retraining.

### 26. [MindEval2: Clinically Grounded Patients and Rubrics for Evaluating Language Models in Mental Health Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22314v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：mindeval2, mental health, evaluate proprietary and open-weight, overly forthcoming and self-reflective, self-reflective and judgments typically, health support the NEED
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22314v1>

**原始摘要**

With people increasingly turning to AI chatbots for mental health support, the need to evaluate these tools is becoming more pressing. Recent benchmarks attempt to mimic a realistic setting by simulating multi-turn interactions with synthetic patients and evaluating them with an LLM-as-a-judge. However, the usefulness of these methods is limited because simulated patients can be overly forthcoming and self-reflective, and judgments typically involve opaque Likert scales with unclear practical utility. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MindEval2, an evaluation framework that consists of two components: Mental Health Quality Rubrics (MHQR), for evaluation, and MindSim, for patient simulation. For MHQR, we collaborate with PhD-level psychologists to develop a set of rubrics that break down response quality into explicit operational criteria that are more interpretable and actionable than existing evaluation taxonomies. MindSim, in turn, is an LLM harness that combines carefully-designed patient profiles with tools, enabling more varied, challenging, and realistic interactions than existing baselines. The harness also contains a memory component that is updated between each interaction, enabling MindEval2 to model the longitudinal aspect of mental health. We evaluate 18 proprietary and open-weight models on MindEval2, finding that while frontier models have largely improved on issues related to surface level quality and boundaries, guidance calibration remains the dominant failure mode across all models, accounting for most of the quality gap.

### 27. [Velox: Spatial Memory Makes World Models Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22311v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：attention, world models, diffusion, velox-world.com velox for MORE, frustum SLAB, https velox-world.com velox
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22311v1>

**原始摘要**

Video world models turn actions and camera motion into long visual rollouts, making video a natural interface for interactive simulation. Such interaction requires responsive generation, yet coherent rollouts must retain visual memory of what has already been generated. Chunked video diffusion models usually keep this context in growing KV caches, so later chunks become progressively slower. To address this bottleneck, we introduce Velox, a training-free attention router for region-level context selection using frustum slab correspondences from streaming geometry. Velox represents historical blocks as camera frustum slabs with depth intervals and obtains current-block depth through recency-priority multiwarp. For well-covered regions, frustum slab overlap softly modulates each layer's model-derived block importance. Uncovered regions retain the model-derived route without geometric bias. The fixed token-block granularity matches FlashAttention-style tiled execution and reduces the attention term that grows with rollout length. Frustum slab construction runs asynchronously with denoising on the same H200. At chunk 48, Velox accelerates attention by $4.36\times$ and denoising by $2.43\times$. On the full 158-case WBench navigation split, it remains within 0.15 API-free average points of dense LingBot-Fast-Cam. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/velox/ for more details.

### 28. [Knowledge That Is Linear Before Fine-Tuning Generalizes After It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22300v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-tuning, held-out subjects that share, original relation, after it. NOT ALL, among the linear probe, often among the linear
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22300v1>

**原始摘要**

Not all facts a language model can recall support further learning equally well. We distinguish active facts, which support generalization beyond their own recall, from inert facts, which can be recalled but do not support such generalization in a given task. We study this distinction by constructing new relations from existing ones. Take the relation that maps a place to its continent (Paris -> Europe) and assign each of its answers an arbitrary new label (Europe -> crab, Asia -> zebra). The new relation then maps Paris -> crab. We fine-tune the model on the new relation for a subset of subjects, without telling it the original relation or the relabeling rule, and test whether it assigns the corresponding labels to held-out subjects that share an answer in the original relation. Having learned Paris -> crab, does it answer Munich -> crab? Across eleven language models and 70 relations, how linearly readable the original relation is in pretrained representations strongly predicts this generalization. Within each model, the two correlate across relations at Pearson r = 0.87 to 0.92. This association persists when controlling, separately and jointly, for the number of fine-tuning subjects and answers, the model's accuracy on the original relation, and how often the subjects occur in pretraining text. It is also robust to re-randomizing the label assignment and fine-tuning seed, and to replacing the animal labels with labels from thirteen other semantic categories. When we map a fine-tuned model's wrong label back to the original answer it was assigned to, Munich -> zebra becomes Munich -> Asia. Answers recovered this way are more often among the linear probe's top answers for the subject than among the pretrained model's own. Whether a new relation generalizes depends less on whether its facts can be recalled than on how readily their shared structure can be read out linearly.

### 29. [Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22295v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：activity recognition, human-attributed description and activity, grounded human-attributed description, attention, human-annotations and exact text-text, recognition single-person activity
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22295v1>

**原始摘要**

Understanding characteristics and activities of individuals in complex multi-person environments is crucial for real-world applications. However, existing datasets often simplify this problem to grounded group activity recognition, single-person activity recognition, or close-set activity recognition, thus limiting the generalizability of models trained on these datasets. In this work, we introduce the task of Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition (GHADAR), which involves describing the characteristics and activity descriptions of \textbf{every} person in a video, provided the location of the person in the video, which is more practical for real-world applications. To facilitate this, we introduce a new dataset derived from AVA-Actions by generating open-set captions for the person description and activity. In addition, we propose a novel method to effectively utilize the information contained in grounding during training by constraining the cross-attention masks during training in VLMs to improve performance for this task. Our experiments show that our method outperforms SOTA VLMs on this task. Finally, we demonstrate the limitations of existing evaluation metrics, which are overly reliant on human-annotations and exact text-text matching. As an added video-based evaluation, we propose a holistic VLM-based evaluation schema that compares concepts {\em directly} between the video and the generated predictions. Thus, in this work, we develop a complete framework for GHADAR, including a dataset, a novel method and an evaluation schema, thereby establishing a strong foundation for future research in this domain.

### 30. [Multi-Target Few-Shot Prompting with Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22294v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：few-shot prompting, prompting with diffusion language, multi-target few-shot, autoregressive, diffusion language models. few-shot, example-supported infills. across few-shot
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22294v1>

**原始摘要**

Few-shot prompting is useful when instructions and rewards are hard to specify. One such application is editing by example, where a demonstrated formatting change or API migration may need to be applied across multiple sites. However, such edits often depend on both left and right context, making generation with causal autoregressive models awkward. We therefore introduce multi-target few-shot prompting with diffusion language models, where examples guide the infilling of multiple masked regions within a shared canvas. To address the known challenge of reliably following demonstrations in few-shot prompting, we further leverage selective masking of the canvas to derive a likelihood-ratio reward that favors example-supported infills. Across few-shot prompting benchmarks and software refactoring case studies, we find that demonstrations improve infilling accuracy, with example guidance yielding further gains in several settings.

### 31. [Towards Continual Learning: Mitigating Forgetting With Subspace Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22293v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, fine-tuning, continual learning, distillation, large language models, learning and on-policy
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22293v1>

**原始摘要**

Large language models (LLMs) acquire new skills through fine-tuning, but it can destroy capabilities they already have: catastrophic forgetting remains a central obstacle to continual learning in LLMs. Recent works connect forgetting to how far, and along which directions, fine-tuning moves the weights. On-policy methods such as reinforcement learning and on-policy distillation avoid updating the principal singular directions of weight matrices and thus forget less. In contrast, SFT concentrates updates along these directions and is susceptible to catastrophic forgetting. In this work, we introduce Subspace Tuning (SST), which restricts each weight update to the bottom singular subspace, eliminating changes along the principal directions. SST is optimizer-agnostic and composes well with fine-tuning recipes including SFT and self-distillation. SST combines tight subspace protection, dense gradient estimation, and a simple refresh mechanism, enabling stronger learning than prior orthogonal fine-tuning methods. With these mechanisms, protecting as much as 99\% of the weight subspace substantially improves retention while preserving strong target-task performance. This behavior strengthens with model and data size and nearly eliminates forgetting in the large-model regime. SST also keeps the model close to its base distribution, inducing $2$--$4\times$ less KL drift than the baseline recipes. Overall, SST advances the learning--forgetting Pareto frontier, making it a practical default for fine-tuning pre-trained language models.

### 32. [EliteReplay: Preserving Diverse Solutions in RL Post-Training via Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22290v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：quality-diversity search., reinforcement learning, post-training recipe that pairs, multiple text-to-sql tasks elitereplay, text-to-sql tasks elitereplay achieves, common post-training technique
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22290v1>

**原始摘要**

Reinforcement learning has become a common post-training technique to improve the accuracy of LLMs on verifiable tasks. However, such training can narrow the set of solutions the model can produce. Several approaches have attempted to address this behavior-sharpening effect, but they often optimize metrics that do not reflect the diversity of correct solutions. Ideally, we want a model that can produce multiple good solutions, not only a single reliable one. We propose EliteReplay, a post-training recipe that pairs GRPO with an "elite" archive built by quality-diversity search. Before training, we use the frozen base model to collect solutions that span distinct approaches; during training, GRPO is combined with a replay loss on this archive. The result is a post-trained model that improves accuracy while preserving more of the base model's solution diversity. On multiple text-to-SQL tasks, EliteReplay achieves a higher pass@128 than all baselines, producing up to 77% more distinct correct strategies than the strongest baselines. It preserves most of the base model's correct strategies, outperforming existing methods in strategy retention. This diversity translates into practical benefits on ambiguous questions, where EliteReplay covers both interpretations nearly twice as often as the strongest baseline on AmbiQT.

### 33. [Improving Environment-Agnostic Coding Agents: The Role of Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22289v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, token-based cost. the experiments, gpt-5.6-sol. the reference prescribes, AGI MAZE, improving environment-agnostic coding, changes. YET the general
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22289v1>

**原始摘要**

Stronger language models may increasingly construct useful procedures for themselves, reducing the advantage of developer-supplied scaffolding. We investigate why this advantage persists and whether an environment-agnostic agent can be improved without environment-specific engineering. We evaluate a Codex-based agent in two nearly identical prompt variants on ARC-AGI-3, AGI Maze, and Terminal-Bench. Each variant is deployed with unchanged code, prompts, and general-purpose tools across environments, receiving environment-specific information as input. Changing only an instruction to encourage reporting difficulties and returning control for automatic continuation improves mean scores on both ARC-AGI-3 and AGI Maze, while Terminal-Bench shows small, mixed changes. Yet the general agent remains below an ARC-AGI-3-specific reference, including with GPT-5.6-sol. The reference prescribes organization and control rather than supplying new game rules or solutions. To investigate this gap, we transfer reset decisions to a scripted controller that follows a convention already given to the agent. This small change recovers part of the specialized agent's advantage. A complementary AGI Maze experiment shows that additional tutorial experience can reduce performance. Together, these findings highlight the distinction between having information and tools available and using them effectively. Supplied procedures still matter for the models tested here. The performance gains also come with higher token-based cost. The experiments do not separate better use of information and procedures from additional or differently allocated inference. We provide the EnvTraverse framework and an evaluated baseline agent for testing whether further environment-independent changes improve performance in some environments without degrading it in others.

### 34. [Learning Rational Strategies for Temporal Objectives in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22287v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, reinforcement learning, rational synthesis, temporal objectives, probabilistic temporal, guarantees for tabular nash-q
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22287v1>

**原始摘要**

We study rational synthesis for stochastic multi-agent systems with unknown dynamics, where each agent is equipped with its own probabilistic temporal objective specified using Probabilistic Linear Temporal Logic on Finite Traces ($\mathsf{PLTL}_f}$). We impose solution concepts (e.g., Nash equilibrium, subgame-perfect equilibrium) as constraints on the joint strategy profile, using payoffs derived from each agent's probability of satisfying its temporal objective. In particular, we formulate per-agent rational synthesis as finding a strategy profile -- if one exists -- such that (i) the designated agent's probabilistic temporal constraint is satisfied, and (ii) the profile represents a stable solution under a chosen solution concept. We give conditional convergence guarantees for tabular Nash-Q learning, certify satisfaction with simultaneous finite-sample confidence bounds, and assess strategic stability with approximate best-response checking. Our approach operates in a stochastic \emph{Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning} (MARL) environment, where model-free learning algorithms attempt to attain extremal satisfaction probabilities for temporal objectives automatically compiled into reward automata. Our approach is empirically validated using 8 general-sum concurrent stochastic games.

### 35. [Asking Smarter Questions: Targeted Active Preference Learning for Underspecified Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22285v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：active preference learning, preference queries, attention, low-effort channel for resolving, informed prior, matters for the task.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22285v1>

**原始摘要**

Reward learning typically assumes the human's input covers everything that matters for the task. In practice, humans can only attend to so much at once, so their teaching naturally captures some features---or aspects of the task---better than others. Prior work has shown how the robot can identify which features were poorly supervised directly from its teaching data. But the current solution is to ask the human to try again, putting the burden on someone who already struggled to convey those features. We propose shifting this burden to the robot. Our key insight is that the same information that tells the robot what it doesn't know can help it ask better questions. Preference queries offer a low-effort channel for resolving this uncertainty, but only if the robot knows where to direct them and the human knows what to attend to when answering. We act on this insight in two ways: we build an informed prior that targets preference queries at the features that need resolving, and we accompany each query with an explanation that focuses the human's attention on exactly those features. In simulation, the informed prior beats the standard active preference learning baseline by up to $30.8\%$ in the low-query regime, where reducing human effort matters most. In a user study, the informed prior and explanations each significantly improve reward recovery and amplify each other.

### 36. [Routing Policy Optimization for Online Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22282v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, attention, routing policy optimization, diffusion, text-to-image, text-to-video
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22282v1>

**原始摘要**

Reinforcement learning can align visual generators with reward signals beyond their pretraining objectives, but existing score-function methods typically introduce stochasticity by perturbing the reverse denoising trajectory, leading to costly rollout-based optimization. We instead ask whether stochastic exploration can be introduced inside the generator while preserving its original sampler. Routing Policy Optimization (RPO) answers this question by turning attention routing into a stochastic exploration action. At a few selected denoising levels, each query block keeps its own block and samples further key blocks in proportion to the generator's native attention mass, or keeps dense attention. The sampled route perturbs the velocity of that transition, and a Gaussian likelihood around the dense velocity scores the perturbation with a score-function objective. RPO further uses a controlled two-rollout estimator: two rollouts share the same prompt, initial latent, routed denoising levels, and dense anchor layers, differing only in their sampled attention routes. Their reward difference provides an advantage signal for the routed transitions. Because the likelihood differentiates the dense velocity itself, the learned updates remain effective after routing is removed. At inference, RPO restores dense attention and retains the original deterministic sampler. Experiments on text-to-image and text-to-video generation show that RPO improves generation quality while achieving nearly 4x higher training efficiency than Flow-GRPO-style methods. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/rpo for visual results.

### 37. [LEASH: Uncertainty-Aware Weak-to-Strong Escalation and Oversight for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22280v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, weak-to-strong escalation, alone often fails. weak-to-strong, rates. with qwen3.5-35b-a3b leash, step-level uncertainty for runtime, escalation rates. with qwen3.5-35b-a3b
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22280v1>

**原始摘要**

Computer-use agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks, where running a frontier model at every step is expensive and a small open-weight model alone often fails. Weak-to-strong escalation offers a middle ground where a weak agent acts by default and hands control to a strong one only when needed. The core challenge is deciding when to escalate, which requires an uncertainty signal cheap enough to check at every step. However, even after handoff, the strong agent can still get lost, but a black-box API that the strong agent is usually served exposes no internals to monitor. To this end, we propose LEASH, a training-free framework that unleashes the strong agent only when the weak agent's latent uncertainty signals it is needed, and keeps it on a leash afterward. LEASH has three layers: (1) initial task uncertainty for early routing, (2) step-level uncertainty for runtime escalation, and, after handoff, (3) a shadow observer for continued oversight of the strong black-box agent. Here, we use latent uncertainty as a fuel for all three layers. Specifically, we compare the weak model's hidden states with those from its past successful and failed trajectories, and a state that looks more like the failures signals higher uncertainty. During weak-agent execution, our uncertainty estimator reuses activations already produced during action generation, so it needs no extra forward pass or dedicated monitor training. Experiments on OSWorld across four contemporary model backbones show that LEASH consistently improves over standalone weak agents and outperforms random escalation by 6.55--11.94 points at matched escalation rates. With Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, LEASH achieves 73.66% task success at $0.4117 per task, matching always-on Claude Opus 5 (72.90%) while reducing inference cost by 19.2%.

### 38. [Auditable and Deployable Multi-Agent Motion Planning via Posetal Games from Legal Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22277v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：multi-agent motion, posetal games, motion planning, auditable and deployable multi-agent, indicate that guideline-based posetal, games scenario-independent legal guidelines
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22277v1>

**原始摘要**

When robots are deployed in the real-world, they must interact with other agents and accomplish their own goals while adhering to legal constraints. These potentially conflicting objectives are often only partially prioritized, making scalarized objectives or fully specified hierarchies unsuitable. We present a framework for multi-agent motion planning under legal requirements based on games with partially ordered preferences (posetal games). Scenario-independent legal guidelines order abstract legal concerns, from which we automatically derive agent preferences and a legal game for any concrete scenario. Agents may refine their legal preferences with private objectives, and regulators may refine the guideline as laws evolve. We prove that such customizations can only remove equilibria and thus preserve legality. To coordinate agents without explicitly communicating private preferences, we introduce belief play, which provably recovers the true equilibria from conservative local games, and a distributed iterative best-response protocol requiring less information. We apply the framework to maritime navigation, systematically lifting pairwise traffic rules to multi-vessel encounters. On seven encounters with up to four vessels, every rule violation of the committed equilibria is certified as forced by the legal hierarchy or unsatisfiable, and defectors are identified from public information in all tested cases. These results indicate that guideline-based posetal games offer an auditable route from partially specified laws to multi-agent motion planning.

### 39. [MJev-Spatial: System One Spatial Intelligence via Native Attention Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22276v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：spatial intelligence, autoregressive, agent, attention, multimodal, vision-language
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22276v1>

**原始摘要**
